## ⚖️ THE FOG CARD (2026-09-03) — namespace **`FOG-§`**

📌 **Jonathan's directive, verbatim, ⛔ once:**

> *"The second card is a 'fog' card. It costs 50 gold and will roll a fog into the map. The fog makes it to where players can really only see up until about 20 feet in front of you. And that includes AI and all units. Meaning that ranged units will not be able to fire beyond this range and players will not be able to see anything beyond this range. I want this fog to look as realistic as possible, so I want the player to begin to see a light amount of fog starting at about 10 feet away and it gets thicker and thicker until they really cannot see anything beyond 20 feet away. I added a 'FogArea' asset to the game. Use that one."*

### FOG-§1 ⭐⛔⛔ **THE UNIT CONVERSION — PINNED ONCE, WITH THE ARITHMETIC SHOWN, SO ⛔ NOBODY RE-DERIVES IT**

> ### **1 ft = 30.48 cm (exact, by international definition).**
> ### **10 ft = 10 × 30.48 = 304.8 cm = ⭐ `304.8 uu`**
> ### **20 ft = 20 × 30.48 = 609.6 cm = ⭐ `609.6 uu`**

- ⛔⛔ **`304.8` AND `609.6` ARE THE ONLY NUMBERS. ⛔ NOT 300/600 ("close enough"), ⛔ NOT 10/20 (feet-as-units).** ⚠️ **Feet-as-units is wrong by ⛔ 30× and ⛔ looks entirely plausible in review** — a `20` in a float named `FogVisibilityUU` reads fine and would put the fog ceiling ⛔ *inside the unit's own capsule*.
- ✅ **They ship as ⛔ TWO named `EditDefaultsOnly` constants carrying the arithmetic in their comments** — **`FogVisionCeilingUU = 609.6f`** and **`FogVisionOnsetUU = 304.8f`** — and ⛔ **no second literal of either exists anywhere in the codebase.**
- ⭐ **THIS IS THE SAME LAW AS `HIGH-§1`'s `152.4`, AND IT IS THE SECOND TIME HE HAS SPECIFIED A DISTANCE IN FEET.** ⇒ ⛔ **feet-in-prose is now a ⛔ recognised project idiom: any future number he gives in feet is converted ⛔ at 30.48 and ⛔ pinned with its arithmetic.**

### FOG-§2 ⭐⭐⭐ **THE CONSEQUENCE HE MAY NOT HAVE COMPUTED — MEASURED, ⛔ AND HIS NUMBER IS ⛔ NOT ADJUSTED**

⛔ **Every figure below is read from `Docs/Data/cards.csv` and `ScatterConfig.h`. ⛔ Nothing is estimated.**

- **The arena is `ArenaHalfExtent = (26000, 12000)` with castles at ±25,000** ⇒ ⭐ **50,000 uu castle-to-castle = 1,640 ft.**
- **`609.6 / 50,000 = 1.22%`** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **under fog a player sees ⛔ one-eightieth of the way to the enemy castle.**

| card | shipped `Range` (uu) | = ft | under a 609.6 ceiling | ⛔ cut |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Longbowman** | **3600** | 118.1 | 609.6 | ⛔⛔ **−83.1%** |
| **Archer** | **2100** | 68.9 | 609.6 | ⛔ **−71.0%** |
| **Wizard** | **2100** | 68.9 | 609.6 | ⛔ **−71.0%** |
| BallistaTower | 1400 (`MinRange` **300**) | 45.9 | 609.6 | ⛔ **−56.5%** ⇒ ⚠️ **usable band collapses to a 300–609.6 annulus** |
| ArrowTower | 900 | 29.5 | 609.6 | −32.3% |
| BombTower / CrystalTower | 800 | 26.2 | 609.6 | −23.8% |
| Cleric (heal) | 400 | 13.1 | — | ✅ **unaffected — already inside the ceiling** |
| every melee unit | 120 | 3.9 | — | ✅ **unaffected** |

- ⭐⭐ **⇒ FOG WOULD BE THE SINGLE LARGEST COMBAT MODIFIER IN THE GAME.** ⚖️ **That may be ⛔ exactly what he wants from a 50-gold card** — ⭐ it converts a ranged siege into a melee brawl in one play, which is a ⛔ genuinely great card. ⛔ **But he should rule on the number ⛔ knowing it.**
- ⛔⛔ **AND THE CLAIM I WAS HANDED IS ⛔ WRONG, SO IT IS ⛔ CORRECTED HERE RATHER THAN REPEATED:** *"20 ft is roughly one-and-a-half hero body-lengths"* is ⛔ **false**. **Measured: the hero capsule is `InitCapsuleSize(42, 96)` ⇒ ⭐ 192 uu tall ⇒ 6.30 ft** (`SiegeGhostPawn.cpp:51-53`, which mirrors the hero's own; `HeroCharacter.cpp:1720` independently pins **96.0**, *"⛔ never 88"*). ⇒ **`609.6 / 192 = ⭐ 3.18 body-lengths`, ⛔ not 1.5.** ⭐ **"One-and-a-half body-lengths" is his ⛔ 10-ft ONSET (`304.8 / 192 = 1.59`) — ⛔ the figure was attached to the wrong end of his own sentence.**
- ✅ **A useful sanity anchor, since it makes the whole scale coherent: the hero is ⛔ 6.3 ft tall, so this project's characters ⛔ are authored at real-world scale, ⛔ which is exactly why `30.48` is the right conversion and `1.0` is not.**

### FOG-§3 ⭐⭐⭐ **WHAT `FogArea` ⛔ ACTUALLY IS — MEASURED FROM THE ASSET, ⛔ NOT ASSUMED**

⛔ **He said *"I added a 'FogArea' asset to the game. Use that one."* ⇒ it was ⛔ located and ⛔ read before a single task was written.** ⭐ **It is ⛔ NOT what the phrase "fog" makes everyone assume.**

**Measured from `Content/FogArea/Blueprints/BP_FogArea.uasset` (name-table + import-table read):**

| property | measured value |
|---|---|
| **what it is** | ⭐ **an ⛔ `AActor` Blueprint (`BP_FogArea_C`)** with a `DefaultSceneRoot` + a ⛔ **`StaticMeshComponent`** |
| **what it renders** | ⛔ **`/Engine/BasicShapes/Cube` or `/Engine/BasicShapes/Sphere`** carrying ~~a ⛔ **raymarched translucent material**~~ ⇒ ⛔⛔ **CORRECTED 2026-09-02 (`TASK-836`, ⛔ measured in-editor): `M_FogArea` is ⛔ `MaterialDomain = MD_Volume`** — it ⛔ **INJECTS Albedo / Emissive / `Extinction` into UE's ⛔ VOLUMETRIC FOG FROXEL GRID.** ⛔ It does ⛔ not raymarch and it is ⛔ not translucent. (`M_FogArea` + 7 `MI_FogArea_*` variants, driven by a `MaterialInstanceDynamic`) |
| **its data** | 6 user structs (`S_FogArea{General,Shape,Noise,Shadows,Shafts,Field}`) + 3 enums; setters `SetGeneralData` / `SetShapeData` / `SetNoiseData` / `SetShadowsData` / `SetShaftsData` / `SetDistanceFieldData` |
| **its knobs** | `Density` · `Base Color` · `Emissive Color` · Base Noise (size/sharpness/channel/**`VolumeTexture`**) · **`Distortion` (intensity/scale/speed/texture)** · `Wind Speed`/`Direction`/`Global Wind` · Shadows · LightShafts · Distance-Field colour/gradient/offset · `MaxDrawDistance` |
| ⛔ **collision** | ⛔ **`ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision`** |
| ⛔ **mobility** | ⛔ **`Static`** |
| ⛔ **engine version** | ⚠️ **`++UE5+Release-5.5`** — ⛔ **a 5.5 asset in a 5.8 project** |

- ✅ **IT IS ⛔ NOT a `UExponentialHeightFogComponent`. ⛔ NOT a Niagara system** (⭐ **so the *"a Niagara system duplicated via MCP is silently inert"* finding ⛔ does not apply — ⭐ good news, ⛔ and it was checked, ⛔ not assumed). **⛔ NOT a post-process volume. ⛔ NOT a `LocalFogVolume`.**
- ⛔⛔⛔ **AND HERE IS THE FINDING THAT MATTERS MOST, BECAUSE IT IS A ⛔ MISMATCH BETWEEN HIS ASSET AND HIS SENTENCE:**
  - ⭐ **`BP_FogArea` puts ⛔ FOG IN A PLACE.** It is a ⛔ **bounded box/sphere volume** with ⛔ **uniform internal density**.
  - ⛔ **His sentence asks for ⛔ FOG AT A DISTANCE FROM THE VIEWER** — *"begin to see a light amount of fog starting at about 10 feet away … until they really cannot see anything beyond 20 feet"* is a ⭐ **camera-relative depth falloff**, ⛔ which is what height fog / a post-process does and ⛔ what a mesh-bounded volume ⛔ does **not**.
  - ⇒ ⚖️ **BOTH READINGS ARE BUILDABLE AND THEY ARE ⛔ DIFFERENT FEATURES.** 🧑 **`J-F1` is the ⛔ single most valuable question in this batch** (`FOG-§5`).
- ⛔⛔ **AND THE FLAT FACT UNDERNEATH ALL OF IT: `BP_FogArea` HAS ⛔ ZERO GAMEPLAY HOOKS.** ⛔ No collision, ⛔ static, ⛔ purely a renderer. ⇒ ⭐ **it can ⛔ never, by itself, stop an archer firing or an AI planning. ⛔ Every gameplay effect in his paragraph is ⛔ new C++ that is ⛔ completely independent of this asset.**
- ⛔⛔⛔ **AMENDED 2026-09-02 BY `TASK-836` — ⛔ THE SIXTH LAW-FACT DRIFT OF THE DAY, AND ⛔ THIS ONE REVERSES A COST CONCLUSION RATHER THAN MOVING A NUMBER** (`SC-§38` cl. 4's shape: ⛔ a law describing ⛔ BEHAVIOUR):
  - ⛔ **`MD_Volume`, ⛔ not raymarched translucency** (row above). ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE ARENA-SCALE COST WORRY RECORDED HERE IS ⛔ BACKWARDS: ⛔ froxel cost is ⛔ VIEW-RESOLUTION-SIZED, ⛔ NOT VOLUME-SIZED**, and the ⛔ noise samples ⛔ ABSOLUTE WORLD POSITION ⇒ ⛔ **it does ⛔ not stretch when the box is scaled.** ⭐ **Scaling a volume to arena size is ⛔ far cheaper than this section implied, and ⛔ a task written to avoid that cost would have been avoiding ⛔ nothing.**
  - ✅ **THE `5.5`-IN-`5.8` RISK IS ⛔ DISCHARGED, ⛔ measured: the asset ⛔ LOADS, ⛔ COMPILES and ⛔ BUILDS CLEAN — ⛔ all 5 textures built (`VT_Noises` 128³ ≈ ⚠️ **81 MB**), ⛔ ZERO upgrade warnings, ⛔ ZERO shader errors.**
  - ⚠️⚠️ **BUT IT ⛔ DIRTIES ITS OWN PACKAGE ⛔ ON LOAD** — the editor autosaved `BP_FogArea_Auto3`. ⇒ ⛔⛔ **EVERY SAVE PROMPT IS ⛔ DECLINED, ⛔ by every agent, ⛔ every time.** ⛔ **A "helpful" save writes a ⛔ VENDOR pack** (`FOG-§6`) ⛔ and there is ⛔ nothing to gain by it.
  - ⭐ **AND THE ⛔ SHAPE OF THE FINDING IS WORTH MORE THAN THE FACT: ⛔ the offline name-table/import-table read in this section was ⛔ good enough to plan a lane on and ⛔ wrong about ⛔ what the thing IS.** ⇒ ⚖️ *⛔ An offline read of a binary asset answers ⛔ WHAT IS IN IT, ⛔ never ⛔ WHAT IT DOES. ⭐ The in-editor measurement was ⛔ boarded as its own task and that decision ⛔ paid for itself twice over.*

### FOG-§4 ⭐⭐⭐ **"AND THAT INCLUDES AI" — ⛔ THE AI DOES NOT HAVE EYES, SO THIS ⛔ MUST BE A REAL MECHANISM**

- ⛔⛔ **MEASURED: `SiegeBotController.cpp` contains ⛔ NO `Perception`, ⛔ NO `LineOfSight`, ⛔ NO `CanSee`.** What it ⛔ does have, twice, is: ⭐ ***"Snapshot alive PLAYER-team (enemy) summoned unit locations ONCE"*** (`:1039`, `:1118`).
- ⇒ ⭐⭐ **THE BOT REASONS FROM A ⛔ PERFECT OMNISCIENT SNAPSHOT OF WORLD STATE. ⛔ It has never had vision to take away.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **"the AI can't see" is ⛔ NOT a rendering change and ⛔ NOT free — it is a ⛔ new filtered-perception layer on the bot, ⛔ and it is the single largest hidden cost in this card.**
- ✅ **RULING — ⛔ SPLIT IT, because the two halves have ⛔ wildly different prices:**
  - ⭐ **(a) UNIT-LEVEL ACQUISITION (cheap, ⛔ and it is what he'll actually feel):** ⛔ every acquisition clamps its effective radius to `min(Range, FogVisionCeilingUU)`. ⭐ **This rides `WITCH-§1`'s funnel for ⛔ free** — ⛔ same nine sites, ⛔ same one chokepoint. ⇒ ⭐⭐ **the two cards ⛔ share their single most expensive piece of engineering, ⛔ which is why they are ⛔ one batch.**
  - ⚠️ **(b) STRATEGIC BOT PLANNING (expensive, ⛔ deferred):** making the bot's snapshot ⛔ lie to it touches ⛔ every rule in `SiegeBotController`. 🧑 **`J-F4` — ⛔ default is ⛔ DEFER**, ⛔ recorded, ⛔ not silently dropped.
- ⛔ **AND THE THIRD THING HE DID ⛔ NOT ASK ABOUT BUT WILL SEE IMMEDIATELY: ⛔ the WAR MAP and the commander's overhead view.** ⛔ **Default: ⛔ UNAFFECTED.** ⚖️ *The map is a ⛔ command instrument, ⛔ not a camera; fogging it would break `MARK-§`, `WM-§` and the LLM assistant's snapshot in one stroke.* 🧑 **`J-F5`.**

It keeps its 900.** | ⛔⛔ **HIS SENTENCE DOES ⛔ NOT ANSWER THIS AND ⛔ IT MUST NOT BE READ AS IF IT DID.** He wrote *"⛔ **ranged units** will not be able to fire beyond this range"* — ⭐ **a ⛔ HERO SPELL IS ⛔ NOT A UNIT.** ⇒ three reasons for the default: **(1)** ⛔ a blind clamp is a ⛔ **−32.3% live nerf** to a shipped, ⛔ already-playtested surface his sentence ⛔ never mentions — ⛔ **an unrequested nerf smuggled in under a fog card is worse than a declared residual**; **(2)** ⭐ **this batch ⛔ ALREADY ruled the sibling case: `WITCH-§2` exempts `ApplyRadialDamage` because *"⛔ a blast is ⛔ not an act of seeing"* — ⛔ a directed line sweep is the ⛔ same category** (the hero ⛔ aims; he does ⛔ not acquire); **(3)** ⛔ it is reversible on ⛔ one word, whereas the nerf ⛔ silently changes a card he has played. ⚠️⚠️ **DECLARED RESIDUAL, ⛔ stated because it is the thing that could flip him: ⛔ under fog the hero can ⛔ hit something at 900 uu that he ⛔ literally cannot see.** ⇒ 📌 **`FOG-§7` is the ⛔ structural half of this ruling and it binds ⛔ regardless of which way he rules** |

### FOG-§7 ⭐⭐⭐ **THE FUNNEL SERVES ⛔ THREE DIFFERENT QUESTIONS — ⛔ AND A BLIND CLAMP AT THE CHOKEPOINT ANSWERS ⛔ ALL THREE WITH ⛔ ONE ANSWER** (added 2026-09-03, from `TASK-837`'s measurement)

⛔⛔ **`WITCH-§1` made ⛔ ONE chokepoint. ⭐ That was ⛔ right — but it means the chokepoint is now the ⛔ shared road for consumers that are ⛔ NOT acts of seeing, and `FOG-§6`'s *"the clamp is applied ⛔ INSIDE `GatherHostileAgents`"* ⛔ read as if they were all one kind.** ⛔ **They are not.**

| # | query kind | sites | fog clamp? | invisibility suppression? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | ⭐ **VISION / ACQUISITION** — "what can this thing ⛔ SEE and shoot at" | ⛔ **the ⛔ FIVE gatherer calls in `ASummonedUnit` (×2 — target select + retarget) · `ATower` (×2) · `AHeroCharacter`** · 📌 *`SummonedUnit.cpp:1663`/`:2208`, `Tower.cpp:220`/`:378`, `HeroCharacter.cpp:551` — ⛔ **ALREADY DRIFTED by `TASK-828`; ⛔ `SC-§38`: ⛔ locate by symbol, ⛔ these numbers are a ⛔ dated hint*** | ✅ **CLAMPED — ⛔ this ⛔ IS the card** | ✅ **SUPPRESSED** |
| **2** | ⛔ **BLAST** — "what is ⛔ INSIDE this explosion" | `SiegeCombatStatics.cpp:36` (`ApplyRadialDamage`) | ⛔ **NOT CLAMPED** | ⛔ **NOT SUPPRESSED** (`WITCH-§2`, `J-W2`) |
| **3** | ⛔ **DIRECTED SPELL GEOMETRY** — "what lies ⛔ ALONG this line / in this shape the caster ⛔ AIMED" | `SpellLibrary.cpp:64` (⛔ **Freeze + Lightning**) · `SpellLineSweep.cpp:133` | ⛔ **NOT CLAMPED** (`J-F9` default) | ✅ **SUPPRESSED** (`WITCH-§2` counts these among its 7) |
| **5** | ⭐⭐ **FRIENDLY BUFF** — "which of ⛔ MY OWN units does this reach" | `SpellLibrary.cpp` **`ResolveAllyBuff`** (⛔ Battle Cry) | ⛔ **NOT CLAMPED** | ⛔ **NEVER SUPPRESSED** (`WITCH-§2` lane 4) |
| **4** | ⛔ **CHEAT LANE** | `USiegeCheatManager`'s roster enumeration · 📌 *`SiegeCheatManager.cpp:130`* | ⛔ **NOT CLAMPED** | ⛔ n/a — ⭐⭐ **REWORDED 2026-09-03 (`SC-§38`, `TASK-848` WARN-2): it is ⛔ ROUTED through the gatherer, ⛔ NOT left as a second enumeration.** ⛔⛔ **The old wording — *"exempt ⛔ BY NAME in a comment"* — described the ⛔ PLAN, and `TASK-828` shipped the ⛔ better thing.** ⚠️ **A task that had faithfully obeyed the stale row would have ⛔ RE-INTRODUCED a second `GetAllActorsWithInterface` and ⛔ failed `WITCH-§1`'s grep gate ⛔ in order to honour the law.** ⇒ ⛔ the ⛔ **NAMED comment survives** as the exemption's ⛔ *rationale*; ⛔ the ⛔ enumeration does ⛔ not |

- ⛔⛔⛔ **ROW 5 IS ⛔ NEW (2026-09-03) AND IT WAS ⛔ NEARLY A SHIPPED DEFECT — ⛔ `TASK-828` CAUGHT IT. ⭐ THE SITE COUNT IS ⛔ NINE; THE ⛔ CONSUMER COUNT IS ⛔ ELEVEN, ⛔ AND THAT GAP IS THE FINDING.**
  ⛔ **`SpellLibrary.cpp`'s ⛔ ONE enumeration fed ⛔ THREE independent team-filter loops** — `ResolveFreeze` (⛔ Frost Nova) · `ResolveTopTargetsDamage` (⛔ Lightning) · ⭐ **`ResolveAllyBuff` (⛔ BATTLE CRY)** — through a file-local helper returning the ⛔ **unfiltered** roster.
  ⛔⛔ **`ResolveAllyBuff` wants ⛔ FRIENDLIES.** ⇒ ⛔ **routing all nine sites through `GatherHostileAgents` would have made ⛔ BATTLE CRY BUFF NOBODY — ⛔ a shipped card ⛔ silently doing nothing, with a ⛔ GREEN SUITE and a commit message reading *"pure refactor"*.**
  ⇒ ⭐ **`GatherFriendlyAgents` is ⛔ NOT scope creep: it is the ⛔ same enumerate-and-team-filter step with the ⛔ term inverted, and it is what keeps the ⛔ grep gate at ONE while `WITCH-§2`'s fourth lane stays a ⛔ NAMED lane rather than an ⛔ unexplained second enumeration.**
  ⚖️ **THE LESSON, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ REAL ONE: ⛔ `WITCH-§0` COUNTED ⛔ ENUMERATIONS AND ⛔ CALLED THEM SITES. ⛔ A REFACTOR IS SIZED BY ⛔ CONSUMERS, ⛔ NOT BY `grep` HITS** — ⭐ **and the ⛔ one enumeration with ⛔ three consumers was the ⛔ only one whose consumers ⛔ disagreed with each other.** ⛔ **`§23` again: the ⛔ base of the measurement was wrong, ⛔ not the count.**

- ⭐⭐ **AND READ ROW 3 CAREFULLY, BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ NON-OBVIOUS ONE AND IT IS ⛔ NOT AN INCONSISTENCY: the ⛔ same site is ⛔ SUPPRESSED for invisibility and ⛔ UNCLAMPED for fog.** ⚖️ **The two cards ask ⛔ different questions of one funnel: invisibility filters ⛔ WHICH ACTORS come back (⛔ per-actor, ⛔ per-viewer); fog clamps ⛔ HOW FAR THE QUERY REACHES (⛔ per-query, ⛔ team-blind).** ⇒ ⛔ **a task that "harmonises" them has ⛔ misread both.**
- ✅ **THE STRUCTURAL LAW, AND IT BINDS ⛔ WHICHEVER WAY `J-F9` IS RULED: ⛔ THE EXEMPTION IS EXPRESSED BY ⛔ WHICH FUNCTION THE CALL SITE CALLS — ⛔ NEVER by a branch inside the funnel that inspects its caller, ⛔ never by a magic list of exempt sites, ⛔ never by an `if` on a call-site enum invented for this.** ⭐ **`FSiegeFogStatics::EffectiveVisionRadius` is the guard: ⛔ a BLAST radius is ⛔ not a VISION radius, so a ⛔ wrong call site ⛔ READS wrong.** (⭐ `TASK-837`'s own recommendation, adopted as law.)
- ⛔⛔ **THE COINCIDENCE THAT MAKES ROW 2 ⛔ UNTESTABLE FROM SHIPPED DATA, AND ⛔ THIS IS THE REAL FINDING:** ⛔ **every AoE radius in the game is ⛔ under the ceiling** — Sapper 250 · BombTower 250 · Wizard 250 · Fireball 300 · FrostNova 350 · BattleCry 400 (measured from `Docs/Data/cards.csv`) ⇒ ⭐ **a blind clamp on blasts is ⛔ INERT TODAY, ⛔ by coincidence of ⛔ current data and ⛔ not by design.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A TEST DRAWN FROM SHIPPED DATA ⛔ CANNOT DISCRIMINATE A CORRECT BLAST-EXEMPTION FROM A BROKEN ONE — ⛔ every row passes either way.** ✅ **⇒ THE EXEMPTION IS ASSERTED WITH A ⛔ SYNTHETIC radius ⛔ ABOVE the ceiling (e.g. `700`), ⛔ never with a shipped one.** ⚖️ *Exactly `SC-§37`'s shape one level up: ⛔ there, a test transcribed from its spec cannot fail; ⛔ here, a test drawn from today's data cannot fail.* ⚠️ **And the day someone ships a 700-uu AoE, ⛔ that card silently becomes fog-dependent unless row 2 is ⛔ structural.**

### FOG-§7a ⭐ **THE FALLOFF SHIPPED AS ⛔ QUADRATIC EASE-IN (`t²`), ⛔ NOT LINEAR — ⛔ downstream specs that assumed linear are ⛔ WRONG** (`TASK-837`, ruled + shipped)

- ✅ **`density(d)` on `[0,1]`: ⛔ `0` at/inside `FogVisionOnsetUU` → `t^FogDensityExponent` where `t = (d − onset)/(ceiling − onset)` → ⛔ `1` at/beyond `FogVisionCeilingUU`.** ⭐ **`FogDensityExponent = 2.f` ships as a ⛔ THIRD `EditDefaultsOnly` tunable** — ⛔ a ⛔ shape, ⛔ not a distance, so it ⛔ cannot carry `FOG-§1`'s 30× trap. *"Make it linear"* is a one-word retune to `1.0`.
- ⭐ **WHY (both halves of his sentence, ⛔ and they both point ⛔ away from linear):** *"a ⛔ **light** amount of fog ⛔ **starting** at about 10 feet"* — an ease-in leaves the onset with ⛔ zero slope; ⛔ **one uu past the onset, quadratic reads `0.0000108` and ⛔ linear reads `0.0033` — ⛔ 300× more, i.e. a ⛔ visible EDGE exactly where he asked for a haze.** And *"it gets ⛔ **thicker and thicker**"* is an ⛔ acceleration, ⛔ not a constant rate (quadratic gains ⛔ 0.25 over the first half of the band and ⛔ 0.75 over the second).
- ⛔ **NOT smoothstep** (eases ⛔ out, flattening at the ⛔ one place his sentence is absolute) · ⛔ **NOT Beer-Lambert** (⛔ concave — ⛔ the exact opposite of "thicker and thicker" — ⛔ and asymptotic, so it ⛔ can never reach the state he named).
- ✅ **THE CEILING IS A ⛔ HARD CUT, ⛔ not an asymptote.** *"really cannot see ⛔ **anything** beyond 20 feet"* is absolute; ⛔ an asymptote reading `0.97` leaves ⛔ 3% visibility, and ⛔ at Longbowman range 3% is a ⛔ lethal shot. ⭐ A hard cut is also the ⛔ only version that is ⛔ assertable exactly.
- ✅ **ACQUISITION ONLY — ⛔ an arrow already in the air ⛔ still lands.** His words cover the decision to ⛔ *fire*. ⭐ Same shipped precedent as `WITCH-§2`'s target-locked projectiles (`Projectile.cpp:352`). ⛔ **Declared residual: a volley fired the instant before fog lands ⛔ still connects** — exposure is ⛔ tiny, because firing is ⛔ already clamped.
- ⛔⛔ **SYMMETRY (`J-F3`) IS ENFORCED ⛔ STRUCTURALLY, ⛔ NOT BY A RUNTIME CHECK: ⛔ NOT ONE FUNCTION IN `SiegeFogStatics` TAKES A TEAM, A VIEWER, A CONTROLLER OR AN ACTOR.** ⇒ ⭐ **an asymmetric fog is ⛔ UNREPRESENTABLE — ⛔ you cannot write the favouritism because there is ⛔ no parameter to branch on.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **DO ⛔ NOT ADD A TEAM PARAMETER TO "SUPPORT" SYMMETRY, and ⛔ DO ⛔ NOT WRITE A *"both teams get the same answer"* TEST — ⭐ it would be ⛔ trivially true and would report ⛔ SAFE forever.** ⚖️ *Note the ⛔ deliberate contrast with `WITCH-§`: the ⛔ invisibility predicate ⛔ DOES take viewer + target team, ⛔ because that feature ⛔ IS asymmetric. ⭐ **The two signatures ⛔ disagree on purpose, and ⛔ that disagreement is the design.***
- ⚠️ **THE BOUNDARY, ⛔ DELIBERATE AND ⛔ NOT A BUG:** at `d == 609.6` ⛔ exactly, `FogDensityAt` returns ⛔ `1.0` (opaque) while `IsVisibleThroughFog` returns ⛔ **true** (still in range). ⭐ The project's shipped acquisition idiom is ⛔ **inclusive** (`SummonedUnit.cpp:1644`, `:1785`, `:1851`, `:1956`, `:2409` — all `<= AttackRange`) ⇒ ⛔ **the clamp substitutes the ⛔ OPERAND of that comparison, ⛔ never its SHAPE.** ⛔ Two different consumers asking different questions at ⛔ one float of measure zero.
- ✅ **AND THE PROPERTY THAT MAKES THE WHOLE THING SAFE TO CALL UNCONDITIONALLY: `EffectiveVisionRadius` returns `RequestedRadiusUU` ⛔ BIT-IDENTICALLY when `bFogActive == false`** — ⛔ no clamp, ⛔ no sanitising, ⛔ not one ulp of drift. ⇒ ⭐ **with fog off the game is ⛔ byte-for-byte the game that shipped, which is ⛔ what keeps any fog regression ⛔ ATTRIBUTABLE TO FOG.**
- ✅⭐⭐ **THE DISAGREEMENT ⛔ SURVIVED AN ⛔ ADVERSARIAL READING — ⛔ MEASURED 2026-09-03 BY `TASK-847`, ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ ONLY REASON THIS BULLET IS WORTH MORE THAN THE ONE ABOVE IT.** ⛔ **The two modules were put under ⛔ ONE pair of eyes ⛔ precisely to catch either acquiring the other's shape, and ⛔ neither did: ⛔ NO team/viewer/controller/actor parameter reached ⛔ any fog function · ⛔ NO distance or time term reached ⛔ any of invisibility's six signatures · ⛔ NEITHER header includes the other · ⛔ and the phantom `ESiegeTeam` returns ⛔ ZERO hits across ⛔ ALL of `Source/` (the shipped enum is `ETeamId`).** ⇒ ⭐ **`FOG-§7a`'s *"the two signatures disagree on purpose"* and `WITCH-§1`'s reason for existing are now true of the ⛔ SHIPPED CODE, ⛔ not merely of the law.** ⚖️ *⛔ A law that has ⛔ survived a real gate is worth more than one that has only been ⛔ written — ⭐ and a ⛔ structural guarantee is the ⛔ only kind that can be ⛔ measured this way at all.*

### FOG-§7b ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ **`FogVisionCeilingUU == 0` BLINDS THE ENTIRE ARMY — AND IT SITS ON THE TUNABLE'S ⛔ OWN SLIDER STOP. ⛔ RULED, ⛔ NOT LEFT IMPLICIT.** (added 2026-09-03, from `TASK-847` WARN-3)

⛔ **THE MEASURED FACT, and the reason it is a ruling rather than a nit:** the sanitiser at **`FSiegeFogStatics::EffectiveVisionRadius`** tests **`Ceiling < 0.f`** (⛔ strict) ⇒ ⛔ **zero PASSES the guard**; and the property's **`meta = (ClampMin = "0")`** makes zero the ⛔ **editor's floor value.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **a designer who drags the slider to its stop gets `min(Range, 0) == 0` for ⛔ every acquisition in the game ⇒ ⛔ ALL COMBAT STOPS WHILE FOG IS UP** — ⛔ the exact outcome the guard's ⛔ own comment says must never happen, ⛔ reached through the ⛔ one input adjacent to the guarded one. 📌 *`SiegeFogStatics.cpp:84` / `h:152` as of 2026-09-03 — ⛔ `SC-§38`: ⛔ locate by symbol.*

| option | ⚖️ verdict |
|---|---|
| ⛔ **document `0` as "blind" — a deliberate capability** | ⛔⛔ **REFUSED.** ⭐ **There is ⛔ no design ask for a blindness mode**, and a value that ⛔ stops the game is ⛔ not made safe by a comment. ⚖️ *`HIGH-§1` says write the consequence beside the number — ⛔ it does ⛔ not say a written consequence licenses a trap on the slider stop.* |
| ✅⭐⭐ **RULED — ⛔ BOTH HALVES, and ⛔ neither alone is sufficient** | **(a)** ⛔ **`ClampMin` RAISED from `"0"` to `"304.8"` — the ⛔ ONSET's own pinned value (`FOG-§1`, 10 ft), ⛔ NOT a new invented number.** ⚖️ *Below the onset the model has ⛔ no band at all — the code ⛔ already degenerates there (`Ceiling <= Onset` ⇒ hard step) ⇒ ⭐ **the slider's floor becomes the ⛔ tightest ceiling that is still a FOG.*** **(b)** ⛔ **the guard widens from `< 0.f` to `<= 0.f`** ⇒ zero joins the ⛔ degenerate class and ⛔ returns the request ⛔ UNCHANGED. |

- ⛔⛔ **WHY (b) IS ⛔ NOT REDUNDANT WITH (a), AND THIS IS THE LOAD-BEARING HALF: ⛔ `ClampMin` CONSTRAINS THE ⛔ EDITOR SPINNER AND ⛔ NOTHING ELSE.** ⛔ **A `.ini`, a Blueprint default, a config override or a line of C++ can ⛔ still write `0`** ⇒ ⭐ **(a) protects the designer; ⛔ (b) protects the GAME.** ⚖️ *Shipping only (a) is exactly the class of fix that looks complete and holds nothing.*
- ✅ **AND IT IS ⛔ NOT A NEW RULE — it is the module's ⛔ OWN totality law applied to the ⛔ adjacent input: ⛔ degenerate inputs fail toward ⛔ NO FOG, ⛔ never toward ⛔ NO VISION** (`FOG-§7a`; non-finite, negative and `Ceiling <= Onset` ⛔ all already do this). ⛔ **Zero was the ⛔ one member of that class that fell through.**
- 📌 **WHERE IT LANDS: ⛔ NOT in `TASK-837` (⛔ passed, ⛔ closed, ⛔ inert — ⛔ nothing consults the module yet). ⛔ It ships with `TASK-838`, ⛔ the task that makes the value live**, and it is a ⛔ **named row on `TASK-850`'s gate.** ⚖️ *A defect that ⛔ cannot fire is repaired ⛔ where it becomes able to fire — ⛔ that keeps the fix ⛔ attributable and keeps a passed gate ⛔ closed.*
- ⚠️ **THE RESIDUAL, ⛔ STATED: a designer may ⛔ still type a legal-but-silly `310` and produce a near-useless fog.** ⛔ **That is a ⛔ tuning mistake ⛔ visible on the first playtest; ⛔ zero was a ⛔ TRAP invisible until the army stopped shooting.** ⭐ **The ruling removes the trap, ⛔ not the ability to tune badly** — ⛔ and it does ⛔ not pretend otherwise.

## ⚖️⭐⭐ **FOG-§9 — JONATHAN'S FULL FOG SPEC + THE `BrightSun` CARD (2026-09-03). ⛔ THIS SECTION ⛔ OVERRULES EARLIER `FOG-§` ROWS WHERE THEY DISAGREE, AND ⛔ IT SAYS WHERE.**

📌 **His directive, ⛔ verbatim, ⛔ once. ⛔ THIS IS THE AUTHORITY; ⛔ every ruling below cites a clause of it:**

> *"I still want the fog to be completely universal when the card is played, meaning that it will cover the entire battlefield when the card is played."*
> *"As far as the 'transmittance at half the distance is locked to the square root of the far value. Tuned to 98% obscured at 20 feet, it forces 86% at 10'. I am fine with tuning it to that, in fact, that is probably best because that probably looks the best, so lets do that."*
> *"Just make it to where when fog is up, no ranged units will be able to fire onto anything above 20 feet away in any direction."*
> *"Lets also add a mechanic where fog is up for exactly 5 minutes when the card is played, unless another card clears the fog."*
> *"This card is called 'bright sun'. This card simply clears the fog and will not allow any new fog to come in for a certain amount of time depending on how high the user's hero character position is. The base amount of time that the 'bright sun' card prevents fog from coming in is 2 minutes, and it increases by 1 minute for every 20 feet up from the ground that the user is. Even when the 'bright sun' fog prevention timer ends, the fog that was cleared still remains clear and the fog would have to be reactivated by another fog card. Lets make 'bright sun' cost 60 gold."*

### FOG-§9.1 ⭐⭐⭐ **TWO STANDING BLOCKERS ARE ⛔ DISSOLVED — AND ⛔ ONE OF THEM ⛔ INVERTS A CONCLUSION WE HAD ALREADY REACHED**

| # | what was blocked | ⇒ his clause | ⇒ the resolution |
|---|---|---|---|

| ✅ **`J-F11`** | ⛔ **which INSTRUMENT — (A) modify a duplicate · (B) `ExponentialHeightFog` · (C) keep it place-based?** | ⛔ same clause | ✅⭐ **(C). ⛔ HIS OWN PACK, ⛔ USED AS THE VENDOR BUILT IT, ⛔ SCALED TO THE ARENA.** ⛔ **And `FOG-§3`'s amendment already measured that arena-scaling is ⛔ CHEAP: ⛔ froxel cost is ⛔ VIEW-RESOLUTION-SIZED, ⛔ not volume-sized, and the noise samples ⛔ ABSOLUTE world position ⇒ ⛔ it does ⛔ not stretch when the box is scaled.** ⇒ ⭐ **the one measurement that looked like bad news is ⛔ what makes his ruling cheap.** |

⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND THE LESSON, ⛔ BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ SECOND TIME THIS SHAPE HAS COST THIS LANE A WEEK: ⛔ `FOG-§3`/`§8` ⛔ DERIVED A ⛔ DESIGN INTENT FROM A ⛔ SENTENCE'S ⛔ ILLUSTRATION.** ⛔ His original *"see 20 feet in front of ⛔ **you**"* was read as ⛔ VIEWER-RELATIVE and an entire instrument ruling was built on it. ⛔ **His clarification: the 10/20-ft detail was *"just so I could ⛔ describe what the fog ⛔ LOOKED LIKE"* — ⛔ a ⛔ DESCRIPTION OF AN APPEARANCE, ⛔ never a mechanic.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A DISTANCE IN A DESIGN SENTENCE IS ⛔ NOT AUTOMATICALLY A ⛔ FRAME OF REFERENCE. ⛔ When a number could be ⛔ VIEWER-relative or ⛔ WORLD-relative, that is a ⛔ QUESTION, ⛔ not a reading*** — ⛔ and it is ⛔ cheaper to ask it than to measure a 149-node material graph to answer it. (⭐ `SC-§45`'s sibling, one level up: ⛔ there the ⛔ prose column disagreed with the ⛔ data column; ⛔ here the ⛔ prose disagreed with ⛔ itself.)

### FOG-§9.2 ⛔⛔⛔ **THE FALLOFF IS ⛔ RE-RULED — HIS TUNING ⛔ DIRECTLY CONTRADICTS THE SHIPPED `t²` CURVE, AND ⛔ THE CURVE LOSES**

- ⛔⛔ **`FOG-§7a` ships `density(d)` = ⛔ EXACTLY `0` at/inside `FogVisionOnsetUU` (304.8). ⛔ HE HAS JUST RULED ⛔ 86% OBSCURED THERE.** ⇒ ⛔ **that is a ⛔ DIRECT CONFLICT, ⛔ not a nuance, and ⛔ his word wins.**
- ✅⭐ **THE NEW CURVE IS ⛔ BEER-LAMBERT, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT HE QUOTED BACK AT US:** `Transmittance(d) = exp(−σ·d)`, with **σ ⛔ DERIVED, ⛔ never typed**, from ⛔ ONE new `EditDefaultsOnly` tunable:
  > ### **`FogTransmittanceAtCeiling = 0.02f`** ⇒ **σ = −ln(`FogTransmittanceAtCeiling`) / `FogVisionCeilingUU`**
  > ### ⇒ σ = 3.912 / 609.6 = **0.0064174 per uu**
  > ### ⇒ `Obscuration(609.6)` = **98.00 %** · `Obscuration(304.8)` = **85.86 %** · `Obscuration(152.4)` = **62.4 %**
- ⭐⭐ **THE `√` PROPERTY HE NAMED IS ⛔ NOT A CONSTRAINT WE OBEY — IT IS A ⛔ THEOREM OF THE MODEL, AND THAT IS ⛔ WHY IT IS THE RIGHT MODEL.** `exp(−σ·d/2) = √(exp(−σ·d))` holds for ⛔ every σ and ⛔ every d. ⇒ ⛔ **there is ⛔ NOTHING to assert about it that could ever fail, so ⛔ DO ⛔ NOT WRITE A TEST FOR IT** — ⛔ it would be ⛔ trivially true and would report ⛔ SAFE forever (⛔ the same trap `FOG-§7a` already names for the symmetry test).
- ⛔⛔ **WHAT DIES: ⛔ `FogDensityExponent` and its ⛔ `t^n` remap. ⛔ RETIRED, ⛔ not left dormant** (`SC-§40` cl. 2 — ⛔ dead surface is not an option). ⛔ **`FOG-§7a`'s three tuning targets (`0.0625` / `0.25` / `0.5625`) are ⛔ SUPERSEDED and ⛔ any task still quoting them is ⛔ quoting a retired curve.**
- ⛔⛔⭐ **WHAT ⛔ SURVIVES ⛔ UNCHANGED, ⛔ AND THIS IS THE LOAD-BEARING SENTENCE OF THE WHOLE SECTION: ⛔ THE ⛔ MECHANIC IS ⛔ NOT THE ⛔ LOOK.**
  - ⛔ **`EffectiveVisionRadius` stays a ⛔ HARD CUT at `FogVisionCeilingUU`.** ⛔ `IsVisibleThroughFog` stays `d <= EffectiveVisionRadius(...)`. ⛔ **Beer-Lambert is ⛔ ASYMPTOTIC and would leave ⛔ 2% visibility forever — ⛔ at Longbowman range 2% is a ⛔ LETHAL SHOT** (`FOG-§7a`'s own argument, ⛔ still correct).

### FOG-§9.3 ⛔⛔⛔ **THE FINDING THAT COULD HAVE MADE HIS CARD DO ⛔ NOTHING — AND IT IS ⛔ MEASURED, ⛔ NOT FEARED** ⚠️⚠️ **READ `FOG-§9.6` FIRST: ⛔ HIS 2026-09-04 AMENDMENT ⛔ DISSOLVES HALF OF THIS SECTION AND ⛔ SHARPENS THE OTHER HALF. ⛔ THE TEXT BELOW IS ⛔ RETAINED, ⛔ NOT CURRENT.**

> ### ⛔⛔ **`AggroRadius` IS A ⛔ FLAT `600.f` FOR ⛔ EVERY UNIT IN THE GAME — AND ⛔ 20 ft IS ⛔ `609.6`. ⇒ ⛔ THE SHIPPED CLAMP CHANGES ⛔ NO UNIT'S BEHAVIOUR ⛔ AT ALL.**

| ⛔⛔ **reference `FogVisionCeilingUU`** | ⛔⛔ **REFUSED — ⛔ AND THIS IS THE HALF WORTH KEEPING.** ⛔ `FogVisionCeilingUU` is `EditDefaultsOnly` ⛔ EXPRESSLY so he can retune it in one word (`J-F2`), ⛔ **and `J-F12`'s OWN NAMED REMEDY was *"lower the ceiling below 600"*.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **the ⛔ documented next tuning move would have ⛔ SILENTLY changed Bright Sun's ladder from *"per 20 ft"* to *"per 16.4 ft"* — ⛔ a different card, ⛔ retuned by nobody, ⛔ with a green suite** |

✅⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND THEN HE AMENDED IT TO ⛔ 50 FEET, AND THE COLLISION ⛔ CEASED TO EXIST.** ⛔ **`50 × 30.48 = 1524`, which appears ⛔ nowhere else in the codebase.**

- ✅ **RULED (2026-09-04, ⛔ superseding the 2026-09-03 ruling): ⛔ FOLLOW THE ⛔ SHIPPED IDIOM. ⭐ `BrightSunHeightStepUU = 1524.f`, ⛔ `EditDefaultsOnly`, ⛔ with `// 50 ft × 30.48 cm/ft` beside it, ⛔ and its test ⛔ re-derives `50.f * 30.48f` from a fixture rather than re-typing `1524.f`** — ⛔ **character-for-character the shape of `HeightBonusStepUU = 152.4f` (`HIGH-§1`) and `FogVisionCeilingUU = 609.6f` (`FOG-§1`).**

- ⚖️ ***⛔ THE LAW THAT SURVIVES BOTH RULINGS, AND IT IS THE POINT: ⛔ TWO NUMBERS THAT ARE EQUAL TODAY ARE ⛔ NOT THE SAME NUMBER. ⛔ The test is ⛔ NEVER "do they match" — it is "⛔ WHEN ONE MOVES, ⛔ MUST THE OTHER?" ⛔ If the answer is no, ⛔ sharing the symbol is a ⛔ DEFECT, ⛔ however DRY it looks.*** ⭐ **`RangedFiringCeilingUU = 2000` and `UnitNoticeRadiusUU = 2000` are the ⛔ VERY NEXT INSTANCE of exactly this — ⛔ see `FOG-§9.6`, where the answer is ⛔ again NO.**

#### ✅⭐⭐⛔⛔ **FOG-§9.6b — HIS ⛔ UNIFICATION RULING (2026-09-04, ⛔ same day, ⛔ after reading the separation argument). ⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ LIVE LAW.**

- ⭐⭐ **ONE ⛔ EFFECTIVE ENGAGEMENT RADIUS, ⛔ APPLIED TO ⛔ EVERY UNIT — ⛔ RANGED ⛔ AND MELEE ALIKE.** ⛔ **He states the melee half ⛔ explicitly, so ⛔ `J-F21`'s *"does this hit melee too?"* is ⛔ ANSWERED: ⛔ YES, ⛔ and ⛔ by name.**
- ✅ ⇒ ⛔⛔ **`FOG-§9.5`'s test — *"when one moves, ⛔ MUST the other?"* — now returns ⛔ YES.** ⇒ ⛔ **SHARING THE SYMBOL IS ⛔ CORRECT, and the ⛔ single clamp is ⛔ HIS DESIGN, ⛔ NOT a conflation.** ⚖️ ***⛔ THE LAW DID ⛔ NOT CHANGE — ⛔ THE ⛔ ANSWER TO ITS QUESTION DID. ⛔ That is exactly how a well-posed law is supposed to behave when the design moves.***
- ⛔ **ONE NUMBER IN SHIPPED SOURCE: ⛔ `UnitEngagementRadiusUU = 2000.f`.** ⛔ **BOTH the acquisition gather ⛔ AND the ranged-firing ceiling ⛔ derive from it.** ⛔ **A ⛔ SECOND `2000` literal anywhere is a ⛔ FAIL** (⛔ `FOG-§1`'s one-literal discipline, ⛔ applied to the new constant).
- ⛔⛔⭐ **BUT ⛔ ONE SYMBOL DOES ⛔ NOT MEAN ⛔ ONE CALL SITE, AND THE ⛔ MELEE CASE ⛔ PROVES IT: ⛔ a Footman's `AttackRange` is ⛔ `120`.** ⛔ Under fog its ⛔ NOTICE drops to `609.6` but its ⛔ FIRING stays `120`, because ⛔ `min(120, 609.6) = 120`. ⇒ ⭐ **the two sites ⛔ still each apply their ⛔ OWN `min` against their ⛔ OWN reach; ⛔ what is shared is the ⛔ CEILING, ⛔ not the result.** ⛔ **Collapsing them into a ⛔ single computed number would ⛔ give every melee unit a ⛔ 609.6 attack range ⇒ ⛔ AUTOMATIC FAIL.**
- ⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ BUILD CONSEQUENCE, WHICH IS ⛔ GOOD NEWS: ⛔ THE ⛔ ALREADY-SHIPPED CLAMP INSIDE `GatherHostileAgents` NOW DOES ⛔ MOST OF THE WORK.** ⛔ **A unit cannot fire on what it never acquired**, so capping ⛔ acquisition delivers the ⛔ firing cap ⛔ for free in the ⛔ ordinary case. ⇒ ⛔ **`TASK-839` remains *"replace exactly ⛔ ONE `return false`."*** ⛔ **The firing seam is ⛔ still needed — ⛔ but for the ⛔ RESIDUAL cases in `FOG-§9.7`, ⛔ not for the ordinary one.**

#### ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **FOG-§9.7 — THE ⛔ ONE CASE WHERE NOTICE AND FIRING ⛔ STILL DIVERGE, AND IT IS ⛔ NOT A NUMBER — IT IS ⛔ TIME.** ✅⚖️ **`J-F23` IS ⛔ CLOSED BY JONATHAN 2026-09-04 — ⛔ THE ANSWER IS ⛔ DROP.**

> ### ⛔⛔ **ACQUISITION HAPPENS ⛔ ONCE. ⛔ FIRING CONTINUES ⛔ ON A TARGET ALREADY HELD.**

> ## ⚖️⛔⛔⭐⭐ **HIS RULING, ⛔ VERBATIM: *"fog should make units ⛔ DROP existing targets if they are ⛔ OUTSIDE the 609 range."***
>
> ### ⭐⭐⛔⛔⛔ **⇒ FOG IS A ⛔ STANDING CONDITION, ⛔ CONTINUOUSLY EVALUATED — ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOT AN ⛔ ACQUISITION-TIME FILTER.**
>
> ⛔ **Write it in ⛔ THOSE TERMS, because that sentence is the ⛔ DIFFERENCE BETWEEN TWO IMPLEMENTATIONS THAT ⛔ PASS IDENTICAL TESTS.**

- ✅⭐⭐ **AND IT ⛔ CONFIRMS THE PROCEEDING DEFAULT THIS SECTION ALREADY CARRIED ⇒ ⛔ NO WORK UNWINDS, ⛔ NO ROW IS RE-SPEC'D BACKWARDS.** ⛔ **The default was ⛔ DROP; the ruling is ⛔ DROP.** ⭐ **Recorded because *"the default held"* is ⛔ evidence about ⛔ HOW the default was derived, and that is worth ⛔ more than the ruling itself.**
- ⛔⛔ **BOTH DIRECTIONS RESOLVE THE ⛔ SAME WAY, ⛔ FROM THE ⛔ ONE RULING:** ⛔ **(i)** a unit that acquired at ⛔ 1500 uu ⛔ BEFORE the fog landed ⇒ ⛔ **DROPS IT** · ⛔ **(ii)** a unit whose ⛔ HELD target ⛔ WALKS OUT past `609.6` while fog is up ⇒ ⛔ **DROPS IT.**

⛔ **Three ⛔ live cases, ⛔ none hypothetical:** ⛔ **(a)** a unit acquired at ⛔ 1500 uu ⛔ BEFORE the fog landed · ⛔ **(b)** its target ⛔ walks out to 1200 ⛔ WHILE it shoots · ⛔ **(c)** a ⛔ commanded-zone retarget acquires ⛔ UNBOUNDED (`SeeingFromUnbounded`) and then fires out to `AttackRange`.

| option | ⛔ what the player sees |
|---|---|
| ✅⚖️⭐ **DROP — ⛔ RULED BY JONATHAN 2026-09-04** | ⛔ **fog is ⛔ DECISIVE: ⛔ everyone loses their target the ⛔ instant it lands** |
| ⛔ ~~**KEEP**~~ ⛔ **REJECTED** | ⛔ ~~fog is ⛔ GRADUAL: ⛔ engaged units ⛔ fight on until the target ⛔ dies or ⛔ moves~~ |

- ✅⚖️ **RULED: ⛔ DROP.** ⛔ **(⛔ The reasoning that produced the default, ⛔ retained because it was ⛔ vindicated:)** ⛔ He describes fog as reducing what units can do ⛔ **while it is up**, ⛔ not as a one-time filter, and ⛔ *"no ranged units will be able to fire onto anything above 20 feet away"* reads as a ⛔ **STANDING CONDITION**, ⛔ not an ⛔ acquisition-time test. ⇒ ⭐ **His ruling used ⛔ the same word the derivation did.**
- ⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ IMPLEMENTATION INSIGHT THAT MAKES THIS ⛔ ONE DECISION RATHER THAN TWO: ⛔ *"DROP vs KEEP"* ⛔ IS ⛔ EXACTLY THE QUESTION *"does the ⛔ FOG TERM APPEAR IN THE ⛔ FIRING GATE, ⛔ or only in the ⛔ ACQUISITION GATHER?"*** ⇒ ⛔ **DROP ⇒ the firing gate reads `GetEffectiveFiringRangeUU()` ⛔ every evaluation. ⛔ KEEP ⇒ acquisition alone, and ⛔ the firing gate keeps raw `AttackRange`.** ⛔ **They are the ⛔ SAME diff.**
- ⛔⛔⛔ **THIS IS ⛔ PRECISELY THE `SC-§37` SHAPE AND IT MUST BE ⛔ SAID TO THE IMPLEMENTER: ⛔ AN IMPLEMENTATION THAT FILTERS ⛔ ONLY ACQUISITION WOULD ⛔ SATISFY EVERY TEST WRITTEN FROM THE ⛔ ORDINARY CASE ⛔ WHILE LEAVING ⛔ ENGAGED UNITS ⛔ SHOOTING THROUGH FOG.** ⇒ ⛔ **the suite MUST hold a target ⛔ FIRST, ⛔ THEN raise the fog, ⛔ and assert the unit ⛔ STOPS. ⛔ A test that raises fog ⛔ before acquiring ⛔ cannot tell the two designs apart.**
- ✅ **THE ⛔ MIRROR CASE, ⛔ ANSWERED BY THE ⛔ SAME RULING: does a ⛔ held target survive ⛔ MOVING OUTSIDE `609.6` with fog up?** ⛔ **Under DROP, ⛔ NO — ⛔ and he ⛔ said so himself: *"if they are ⛔ outside the 609 range"* is ⛔ present-tense and ⛔ says nothing about ⛔ when they got there.** ⛔ **One ruling, ⛔ both directions.**

##### ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **FOG-§9.7a — THE ⛔ MELEE CASE IS THE ⛔ SHARPEST INSTANCE, AND IT IS THE ⛔ ONE MOST LIKELY TO BE ⛔ MISSED — BECAUSE *"FIRING"* LANGUAGE DOES ⛔ NOT OBVIOUSLY COVER A ⛔ CHARGE**

- ⛔ **He ruled (`J-F21`, ⛔ by name) that `609.6` binds ⛔ NON-RANGED units too.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A ⛔ MELEE unit ⛔ MID-CHARGE across `1500` uu ⛔ MUST ALSO DROP.**
- ⛔⛔⛔ **AND HERE IS WHY THAT ONE CASE ⛔ DECIDES THE ⛔ SHAPE OF THE WHOLE DIFF: ⛔ A FOOTMAN'S `AttackRange` IS `120`, SO `min(120, 609.6) = 120` ⛔ ALWAYS.** ⇒ ⛔ **THE ⛔ FIRING GATE ⛔ CAN NEVER EXPRESS THE MELEE DROP — ⛔ NO VALUE OF THE FOG TERM MAKES A `120`-RANGE GATE FIRE OR NOT FIRE AT `1500` uu; ⛔ the unit is not firing, ⛔ it is WALKING.**
- ⇒ ⭐⭐⛔ **THEREFORE DROP ⛔ CANNOT LIVE IN THE FIRING GATE ALONE. ⛔ THE FOG TERM MUST APPEAR IN THE ⛔ TARGET-RETENTION PATH — *"⛔ do I ⛔ STILL hold this target?"* — ⛔ evaluated against the ⛔ ENGAGEMENT radius (`609.6` under fog), ⛔ NOT against `AttackRange`.** ⛔ **An implementation that routes ⛔ only the firing gate ⛔ fixes ranged units and ⛔ LEAVES THE MELEE CHARGE ⛔ FULLY INTACT — ⛔ and every ranged-only test still ⛔ passes.**
- ⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ CHEAPEST CORRECT SEAM IS ⛔ ALMOST CERTAINLY ⛔ ALREADY THERE: ⛔ the ⛔ per-tick validity path that ⛔ already drops ⛔ DEAD or ⛔ INVALID targets.** ⇒ ⛔ **`TASK-978` ⛔ MEASURES whether it exists and ⛔ WHERE. ⛔ REUSING it is worth ⛔ far more than adding a ⛔ NEW tick** (⛔ a new poll is ⛔ new cadence, ⛔ new cost, ⛔ new ordering bugs). ⛔ **The implementer gets the ⛔ SEAM, ⛔ never a ⛔ suggestion.**
- ⚠️ **DECLARED RESIDUAL under DROP, ⛔ unchanged from `FOG-§7a`: an ⛔ ARROW ALREADY IN THE AIR ⛔ still lands.** ⛔ **The gate is the ⛔ decision to fire, ⛔ never the projectile.**

#### ⚠️⚠️ **THE ⛔ TWO COSTS OF `600 → 2000` THAT ARE ⛔ NOT ABOUT FOG AT ALL — ⛔ NAMED, ⛔ BECAUSE A REGRESSION MUST TRACE TO THIS ROW AND ⛔ NOT TO THE CARD**

1. ⛔⛔ **IT CHANGES ⛔ EVERY UNIT, ⛔ INCLUDING ⛔ MELEE.** ⛔ `AggroRadius` is ⛔ NOT ranged-only — it is `ASummonedUnit`'s, ⛔ overridden only by `AMinerUnit` and `ASorcererUnit` (⛔ both to `0`). ⇒ ⛔ **engagements start ⛔ 3.33× further out, units ⛔ commit earlier, and a ⛔ melee unit now charges from ⛔ 2000 uu.** 🧑 **`J-F21` — ⛔ his sentence says *"units"*, ⛔ unqualified, so the ⛔ proceeding default is ⛔ YES, ALL UNITS. ⛔ But it is ⛔ flagged, because it is a ⛔ very different game.**

   ⛔ **The ⛔ ENUMERATION delta is `1.00×`. ⛔ The ⛔ TOTAL GATHER COST IS ⛔ NOT UNCHANGED.** ⛔ **At `AggroRadius = 600` the funnel's ⛔ FOG CUT IS ⛔ PROVABLY SKIPPED (`SiegeCombatStatics.cpp:207` — ⛔ `600 < 600` is ⛔ false). ⛔ At `2000` it ⛔ RUNS: ⛔ `ActorGetDistanceToCollision` ⛔ per candidate, ⛔ per unit, ⛔ at 4 Hz under fog — ⛔ work that has ⛔ NEVER executed on this path in this game.**

### ⛔⛔⭐⭐ FOG-§9.8 — **THE COMBAT-LANE MEASUREMENTS (`TASK-978`) + ⛔ THE COMMANDED-LANE RULING. ⛔ FIVE FINDINGS, ⛔ THREE OF WHICH CONTRADICT A BOARDED SPEC.**

#### ⚖️⛔⛔⭐⭐ **FOG-§9.8a — `J-F23`'s ⛔ LAST GAP IS ⛔ CLOSED: ⛔ AN ⛔ ORDER IS ⛔ NOT A ⛔ TARGET** (2026-09-04)

> 📌 **HIS WORDS: *"commanded units ⛔ do not lose their commands in fog, however if an enemy unit walks into the circle that a commanded unit is supposed to be guarding but that enemy unit is ⛔ outside the range in which they can notice them due to ⛔ fog or anything, the commanded unit ⛔ still will not be able to detect them."***

- ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THE LAW, IN THE ⛔ ONLY TERMS THAT KEEP IT STRAIGHT: ⛔ THE CLAMP ACTS ON ⛔ TARGET ACQUISITION AND ⛔ TARGET RETENTION. ⛔ IT ⛔ NEVER ACTS ON THE ⛔ ORDER ASSIGNMENT.** ⛔ **They are ⛔ two different objects and the ⛔ entire ruling turns on ⛔ not conflating them.**
  - ⛔ **The COMMAND ⛔ SURVIVES fog untouched.** ⛔ A unit told to guard a circle ⛔ stays assigned. ⛔ Fog ⛔ does not clear, ⛔ cancel or ⛔ reassign orders.
  - ⛔ **DETECTION is ⛔ STILL CLAMPED.** ⛔ An enemy walking into the ⛔ very circle the unit is guarding is ⛔ UNNOTICED beyond the notice radius.
- ⛔⛔ **THE FAILURE MODE THIS ⛔ FORBIDS, AND IT IS THE ⛔ TEMPTING ONE — ⛔ NAME IT ON EVERY ROW THAT TOUCHES THE LANE:** ⛔ `TASK-978` §4(a) measured that **`UpdateStateGrouped` has ⛔ NO distance-drop path ⛔ at all, ⛔ deliberately** (`SummonedUnit.cpp:1988`, ⛔ quoted: *"There is deliberately NO LeashRange here: the zones ARE the leash for HOLD"*). ⇒ ⛔⛔ **AN IMPLEMENTER TOLD *"FOG DROPS TARGETS"* MAY ADD THE DROP TO THAT LANE ⛔ WHOLESALE ⇒ ⛔ UNITS ⛔ ABANDON THEIR GUARD CIRCLES THE INSTANT FOG LANDS AND ⛔ WANDER OFF THEIR ORDERS.** ⛔ **His ruling says the ⛔ OPPOSITE: they ⛔ HOLD POSITION AND ⛔ GO BLIND.**
- ⇒ ⛔ **The missing expression ⛔ IS added to that lane — ⛔ acting on ⛔ TARGETS ONLY, ⛔ never on the assignment.** ⭐ **Reuse the ⛔ existing leash sites (`:1713`, `:1943`) as `TASK-978` §4(c) recommends; ⛔ do ⛔ NOT add a tick.**
- ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND *"due to fog ⛔ OR ANYTHING"* ⛔ GENERALISES — ⛔ THIS IS A PROPERTY OF THE ⛔ ENGAGEMENT RADIUS, ⛔ NOT OF THE ⛔ FOG CARD.** ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ SAME rule binds at ⛔ `2000` in ⛔ CLEAR weather: a commanded unit ⛔ does not detect an intruder in its own circle ⛔ beyond its notice radius, ⛔ fogged or not.**
  ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE BOARDING SPLIT THAT FOLLOWS, AND IT IS ⛔ LOAD-BEARING: ⭐ `TASK-979` (the `600 → 2000` row) owns the ⛔ GENERAL FORM; ⭐ `TASK-980` owns ⛔ ONLY the fogged value.** ⛔ **If the commanded-lane bound were boarded on the ⛔ fog row alone, ⛔ clear-weather behaviour would ⛔ silently differ from fogged behaviour** — ⛔ the ⛔ exact divergence class this section exists to prevent.
- ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **MEASURED CONSEQUENCE, ⛔ AND HE HAS ⛔ NOT SEEN THIS NUMBER: ⛔ TODAY THE COMMANDED LANE HAS ⛔ NO NOTICE BOUND ⛔ AT ALL.** `AcquireEnemyNearPoint` (`SummonedUnit.cpp:2362`) gathers with ⛔ **`SeeingFromUnbounded`** (`TNumericLimits<float>::Max()`) and filters ⛔ only by ⛔ ZONE MEMBERSHIP (⛔ a 2-D disc, `:1998-2000`). ⇒ ⛔ **his ruling ⛔ INTRODUCES a bound where there was none.**
  ⛔ **And `USiegePlayerController::GroupRadiusMax = ⛔ 5000.f` (`SiegePlayerController.h:2076`) ⇒ ⛔⛔ A ⛔ LEGAL MAX-SIZE GUARD CIRCLE IS ⛔ 2.5× WIDER THAN THE `2000` NOTICE RADIUS.** ⇒ ⛔ **a unit at its centre is blind to the ⛔ `2000..5000` annulus = ⛔ 84% OF ITS OWN CIRCLE'S AREA, ⛔ IN CLEAR WEATHER, ⛔ WITH NO FOG ON SCREEN.** ⛔ **This is ⛔ exactly what he ruled; ⛔ it is ⛔ also a number he ⛔ has not been shown** ⇒ 🧑 **`J-F28`.** ⛔ **Build it; ⛔ state it; ⛔ one word retunes it.**
- ⇒ ✅ **`J-F23` IS ⛔ WHOLLY CLOSED. ⛔ Towers + Siege were already free (they ⛔ hold nothing); ⛔ Standard is covered by the general drop; ⛔ Commanded is now ruled.** ⛔ **Nothing on the DROP ruling remains open.**

#### ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **FOG-§9.8b — `TASK-979` ⛔ SILENTLY NEUTERS `LeashRange`. ⛔ INVISIBLE TO ANY TEST THAT ONLY ASSERTS THE CDO DEFAULT.**

- **`SummonedUnit.h:1043` — `float LeashRange = 900.f;`** *"a target beyond this distance is dropped and Advance resumes"*. ⛔ Read at ⛔ exactly two sites: `:1713` (`UpdateState`) and `:1943` (`UpdateStateStandardCommanded`).
- ⛔ **TODAY `LeashRange (900) > AggroRadius (600)`, so the leash ⛔ IS a leash:** a target chased past 900 is released and ⛔ cannot be re-acquired, ⛔ because re-acquisition only reaches 600.
- ⛔⛔ **AFTER `600 → 2000` THE ORDERING ⛔ INVERTS**, and `UpdateState` runs ⛔ drop-then-reacquire ⛔ in one pass:
```
:1713   if (dist > LeashRange 900)   CurrentTarget = nullptr;   // released
:1721   if (AActor* A = AcquireTarget())  CurrentTarget = A;     // re-acquired, radius now 2000
```
  ⇒ ⛔ **a target at 1,500 uu is ⛔ dropped and ⛔ immediately re-taken in the ⛔ SAME 0.25 s poll. ⛔ The leash stops releasing anything it can still see** ⇒ ⛔ **units ⛔ never disengage.**
- ⭐⭐ **IT IS ⛔ NOT A BUG IN THE DIFF — ⛔ IT IS A ⛔ CONSEQUENCE OF THE NUMBER, and it is ⛔ INVISIBLE TO ANY TEST THAT ONLY ASSERTS `AggroRadius == 2000` ON THE CDO.** ⚖️ ***⛔ A CONSTANT'S BLAST RADIUS IS THE SET OF ⛔ OTHER CONSTANTS IT IS ⛔ ORDERED AGAINST — ⛔ and that ordering is ⛔ nowhere in the type system.***
- ✅ **PROCEEDING DEFAULT: ⛔ PRESERVE THE ⛔ ORDERING, ⛔ not the value.** ⛔ **The leash only ⛔ means anything while it ⛔ exceeds the re-acquire reach; ⛔ the ordering ⛔ IS the shipped design and ⛔ his number ⛔ forces the value.** ⇒ 🧑 **`J-F27`** — ⛔ **`TASK-979` ⛔ states the new number and its consequence beside it (`HIGH-§1`).**
- ⭐ **ONE PLACE IT ⛔ INVERTS AGAIN AND WORKS ⛔ FOR us: ⛔ under fog, the leash drops at `:1713` and re-acquisition at `:1721` is ⛔ fog-clamped to `609.6` and ⛔ FAILS** ⇒ ⛔ **the drop ⛔ STICKS, ⛔ for free, ⛔ with no new code** (`TASK-978` §4(b)).
- ⚠️ **THE BAND NOBODY HAD NAMED: `(609.6, 900]`.** ⛔ A Standard unit holding a target at ~700 uu is ⛔ INSIDE the leash (⛔ not dropped) and ⛔ OUTSIDE the fog ceiling (⛔ must not fire). ⇒ ⛔ **this band is the ⛔ only place Standard units need ⛔ new code.**

#### ⛔⛔ **FOG-§9.8c — `bRanged` IS THE ⛔ PROJECTILE-DELIVERY FLAG, ⛔ NOT AN *"IS RANGED"* FLAG. ⛔ A LIVE TRAP.**

- ⛔ **`CrystalTower` ships `bRanged = ⛔ FALSE`, `Range = 800`, `ChainTargets = 3`.** ⛔ A chain tower ⛔ zaps instantly and ⛔ fires no projectile, so the column is ⛔ legitimately false. ⛔ **`FOG-§2`'s older table is ⛔ correct; the `bRanged` census ⛔ missed it for a ⛔ real reason.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **IF THE FIRING LANE (⛔ or the `2000` ceiling) IS EVER GATED ON `bRangedAttack`, ⛔ `CrystalTower` ⛔ ESCAPES IT** — ⛔ it would keep firing to 800 uu under fog while ⛔ every other tower is cut to 609.6.
- ✅ **SAFE TODAY ⛔ ONLY BECAUSE the tower lane is gated by the ⛔ FUNNEL, ⛔ not by `bRanged`.** ⇒ ⛔ **`FOG-§7`'s *"the exemption is expressed by ⛔ WHICH FUNCTION the call site calls"* is ⛔ what is protecting us here** — ⛔ **the moment anyone writes `if (bRangedAttack)` in the firing lane, ⛔ this card is the hole.**

#### ✅ **FOG-§9.8d — `J-F22` IS ⛔ ANSWERED BY MEASUREMENT. ⛔ NO CODE CHANGE.**

- **`ASummonedUnit::GetDistanceToTarget` (`SummonedUnit.cpp:5385`) is ⛔ 3-D**: `ActorGetDistanceToCollision(...)` with a `FVector::Dist` fallback. ⛔ **No `Size2D()`, no `.Z = 0`, no `DistXY`.** ⛔ **The fog funnel's own cut (`SiegeCombatStatics.cpp:230`) uses the ⛔ IDENTICAL call** ⇒ ⛔ the two metrics ⛔ agree term for term.
- ⇒ ⭐ **A unit on a ×2 Watch Tower (⛔ deck 2,400 uu) is ⛔ 2,400 uu from a target directly below ⇒ ⛔ far outside 609.6 ⇒ ⛔ IT ⛔ CANNOT SHOOT DOWN THROUGH THE FOG.** ⛔ **That is ⛔ already what he described (*"in any direction"*).** ⇒ ⛔ **`J-F22` needs ⛔ NO code change; ⛔ report it, ⛔ do not alter it.**
- ⚠️ **RIDER: `UpdateStateGrouped`'s ⛔ ZONE tests are explicitly ⛔ 2-D discs** (`:1998-2000`, `:2370`). ⇒ ⛔ **the game ⛔ deliberately mixes metrics, so `J-F22` is answered as *"the ⛔ RANGE test is 3-D; the ⛔ ZONE test is 2-D"*, ⛔ NOT as one global answer.**

#### ⚠️⛔ **FOG-§9.8e — `Docs/Data/cards.csv`'s ⛔ FIRST COLUMN HEADER IS ⛔ EMPTY. ⛔ AN INSTRUMENT HAZARD, ⛔ NOT A DEFECT TO FIX.**

- ⛔ **The `CardID` column is ⛔ UNNAMED.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A Python `csv.DictReader` keyed on `'CardID'` returns `''` for ⛔ every row and prints a table of ⛔ blanks that ⛔ LOOKS WELL-FORMED.** ⛔ `TASK-978` §2 hit this and ⛔ caught it; ⛔ the first read had ⛔ already produced the corrupt table.
- ⇒ ⛔ **ANY agent re-deriving a card table ⛔ MUST key on ⛔ `list(row.keys())[0]`, and ⛔ MUST run a ⛔ positive control (`SC-§39`) that a ⛔ known `CardID` comes back ⛔ non-empty.**
- ⛔⛔ **DO ⛔ NOT *"FIX"* THE HEADER.** ⛔ **UE's DataTable CSV importer conventionally treats the first column as the ⛔ RowName and a blank header is the ⛔ normal shape for it** — ⛔ **stated as the ⛔ likely reason, ⛔ NOT as a verified fact.** ⇒ ⛔ **changing it risks ⛔ breaking `DT_Cards`' import for a ⛔ cosmetic gain.** ⛔ **This is a ⛔ `SC-§56`-family entry: ⛔ the hazard is ⛔ recorded so the ⛔ instrument is controlled, ⛔ not so the ⛔ data is edited.**

> 📌 **HIS WORDS, ⛔ AS REVISED 2026-09-04 ⛔ BEFORE ANYTHING WAS DISPATCHED: *"lets keep it at ⛔ 3600 for the ⛔ firing range for the longbowman and make the ⛔ 3600 the ⛔ NOTICE range for the longbowman. ⛔ All other units still only have a notice range of ⛔ 2000."***

##### ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **IT IS ⛔ NOT A NUMBER CHANGE. ⛔ THE CHANNEL IT NEEDS ⛔ DOES NOT EXIST.**

- ⛔ **`SummonedUnit.h:1037` states in terms: *"Acquisition radius in units (GDD §3.8 profile constant: 600 — ⛔ NOT A CARD STAT)"*, and `AggroRadius` at `:1039` is ⛔ NEVER assigned from the card row.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THERE IS ⛔ TODAY ⛔ NO PER-UNIT NOTICE RANGE. ⛔ THE CHANNEL ⛔ IS THE WORK.**
- 📋 **RULED — ⛔ the channel is a ⛔ CARD-ROW COLUMN**, bound in `LoadStatsAndStart` beside the shipped `AttackRange = Row->Range` (`:1288`). ⛔ **He named a ⛔ CARD; *"Data-driven card stats (GDD §3.0)"* is the house law; and a ⛔ future ranged card then inherits the behaviour from its ⛔ own row with ⛔ zero code.**
- ⛔ **REJECTED, ⛔ NAMED SO THEY ARE NOT RE-PROPOSED:** ⛔ **(a) a Blueprint CDO override** — ⛔ `AggroRadius` is `EditAnywhere` so it ⛔ would work, and that is ⛔ precisely the danger: ⛔ un-greppable, ⛔ un-diffable, ⛔ untestable without loading the asset, and ⛔ unable to carry its `HIGH-§1` consequence comment · ⛔ **(b) a C++ subclass** — ⛔ `AMinerUnit`/`ASorcererUnit` set `0.f` as a ⛔ CLASS CONTRACT, ⛔ not as tuning; ⛔ a subclass for ⛔ one number is ⛔ class-tier surface for a ⛔ data problem.
- ⚠️⛔ **STANDING HAZARD, ⛔ RECORDED: a Blueprint ⛔ CAN still override `AggroRadius` and would ⛔ SILENTLY WIN over the C++ default.** ⛔ **`TASK-978`'s *"no Blueprint overrides"* scan was ⛔ POINT-IN-TIME, ⛔ not a guarantee.**
- ⛔⛔ **WHEREVER THE READ LANDS, IT READS THE ⛔ INSTANCE'S CLASS, ⛔ NEVER THE BASE CDO.** ⛔ **`Building.cpp:302` (`TASK-956`) is this project's ⛔ already-shipped instance of exactly that defect.**

##### ⛔⛔⛔ **THE SILENT-FAILURE SHAPE — ⛔ A ⛔ GREEN TEST ⛔ ASSERTS THE DEFECT**

- ⛔ **His ⛔ earlier sentence was *"make that 2000 unit distance the ⛔ MAX range that all ranged units can fire at"*.** ⇒ ⛔ **an implementer who reads that as a ⛔ CEILING writes `min(Range, 2000)` ⇒ ⛔⛔ THE LONGBOWMAN'S `3600` IS ⛔ SILENTLY CLAMPED TO `2000` — ⛔ a ⛔ −44.4% NERF TO THE CARD HE ⛔ JUST EXPLICITLY PROTECTED. ⛔ Nothing errors. ⛔ Nothing logs. ⛔ AND A TEST ASSERTING *"no unit exceeds 2000"* IS ⛔ GREEN AGAINST IT.**

| ⛔ **FOG** | ⛔⛔ **a ⛔ SHARED CEILING of `609.6`, ⛔ APPLIED AS A `min`, to ⛔ NOTICE ⛔ AND ⛔ FIRING, for ⛔ EVERY unit** | | |

#### ⛔⛔⛔ **FOG-§9.10a — THE ⛔ HIGHEST-CONSEQUENCE QUIET BREAK AVAILABLE IN THIS LANE. ⛔ READ IT AS A ⛔ PROHIBITION, ⛔ NOT AS ADVICE.**

- ⛔⛔ ~~**HIS STANDING RULE IS *"notice range is always identical to firing range ⛔ FOR RANGED UNITS."*** ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **THAT RULE ⛔ DELIBERATELY DOES ⛔ NOT EXTEND TO ⛔ NON-RANGED UNITS.**~~
- ⛔⛔⛔ **RETIRED 2026-09-04 BY HIS ⛔ `5000` RULING (⭐⭐⭐ `FOG-§9.11`). ⛔ THE IDENTITY RULE IS ⛔ GONE FOR ⛔ EVERY CLASS: ⛔ `Longbowman 5000/3600` · ⛔ `Archer 5000/2100` · ⛔ `Wizard 5000/2100`.**
- ⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ PROHIBITION IN THIS SECTION IS ⛔ THEREBY ⛔ STRONGER, ⛔ NOT WEAKER — ⛔ read the rest of it as ⛔ FULLY LIVE.** ⛔ **There is now ⛔ NO unit class, ⛔ ranged or melee, for which collapsing notice into firing is ⛔ even approximately right** ⇒ ⛔ the ⛔ one reading that used to make the collapse ⛔ look defensible is ⛔ gone, and ⛔ anyone who still performs it has ⛔ no cover at all.
- ⛔ **A melee unit ⛔ notices at `2000` and ⛔ attacks at `120` ⛔ BY DESIGN. ⛔ THE GAP ⛔ IS THE MECHANIC — ⛔ it must notice ⛔ FAR in order to ⛔ CHASE.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **IF AN IMPLEMENTER ⛔ OVER-APPLIES THE IDENTITY RULE HERE, ⛔ OR ⛔ COLLAPSES NOTICE AND FIRING INTO A ⛔ SINGLE VALUE INSTEAD OF TREATING `609.6` AS A ⛔ SHARED CEILING ⛔ APPLIED AS A `min`, THEN ⛔ EVERY MELEE UNIT IN THE GAME GETS A ⛔ `609.6` ATTACK RANGE UNDER FOG.**
- ⛔⛔ **`min(120, 609.6) = 120` IS THE ⛔ CORRECT SEMANTICS AND IT MUST STAY A ⛔ `min` AT ⛔ EVERY SITE.** ⛔ **Not an assignment. ⛔ Not a *"clamp to the fog value"*. ⛔ A `min`.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ THE DEFECT IS ⛔ SILENT, ⛔ GAME-WIDE, AND ⛔ LOOKS LIKE CONSISTENCY — ⛔ which is ⛔ exactly why it is the one to guard.*** ⛔ **A footman that suddenly swings from 5× its reach would read as a ⛔ physics bug, ⛔ not a range bug, and would be ⛔ hunted in the wrong file.**

#### ⚠️⛔ **FOG-§9.10b — TWO CARDS THE NOTICE BINDING MUST ⛔ NOT MISREAD**

- ⛔ **`Cleric` (heal `400`) and `Witch` (veil `400`) are ⛔ NON-RANGED, and their `400` is ⛔ NOT AN ATTACK RANGE — ⛔ `cards.csv` line 33's own `Notes` cell says so ⛔ in terms.**
- ⛔ **Both sit ⛔ UNDER `609.6`, so ⛔ fog leaves them untouched.** ⇒ ⛔ **the risk is ⛔ NOT the ceiling — it is a ⛔ notice binding that assumes *"`Range` means attack range"* and ⛔ drags them to `2000`, or worse, ⛔ routes their `400` through a firing clamp** (`FOG-§9.8` site 9: ⛔ **the veil read is the ⛔ COMMON case for a fresh witch, ⛔ not an edge case**).

#### ⚠️⛔⭐ **FOG-§9.10c — THE MINER, AND A ⛔ LAUNDERING RISK WORTH NAMING**

### ⚖️⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ FOG-§9.11 — **THE ENGAGEMENT ORDERING LAW (2026-09-04). ⛔ IT ⛔ SUPERSEDES THE NUMBERS IN `FOG-§9.9` AND `FOG-§9.10`, AND IT ⛔ RETIRES `FOG-§9.10a`'s IDENTITY RULE. ⛔ CITE ⛔ THIS, ⛔ NEVER THE TABLE ABOVE.**

#### ⛔ **THE THREE NUMBERS, AND ⛔ NOTHING ELSE MOVED**

| symbol | ⛔ was | ✅ **is** | ⛔ what it means |
|---|---|---|---|
| `UnitEngagementRadiusUU` | ⛔ `2000.f` | ✅ **`5000.f`** | ⛔ the **CLASS DEFAULT** notice radius — ⛔ still a ⛔ DEFAULT, ⛔ **NEVER A CAP** (`FOG-§9.9` — ⛔ that clause ⛔ SURVIVES the number change ⛔ intact) |
| `LeashRange` | ⛔ `900.f` | ✅ **`8000.f`** | ⛔⛔ the **FLOOR** the derivation takes a `max` against — ⛔ **NOT the leash** |
| fog ceiling | ⛔ `609.6` | ✅ **`609.6` — ⛔ UNCHANGED** | ⛔ still a ⛔ SHARED CEILING applied as a ⛔ `min` |

#### ⛔⛔⛔ **THE DERIVATION IS ⛔ LOAD-BEARING AND ⛔ MUST SURVIVE. ⛔ DO ⛔ NOT HARD-CODE A BARE `8000`.**

- ✅ **KEEP `ResolveEffectiveLeashRangeUU(LeashRangeUU, NoticeRadiusUU, MarginMultiplier)` = `max(LeashRange, Notice × Multiplier)` ⛔ EXACTLY AS `TASK-979` BUILT IT.** ⛔ **His `8000` sets the ⛔ FIRST TERM. ⛔ It does ⛔ not replace the expression.**
- ⛔ **TODAY:** `max(8000, 5000 × 1.5 = 7500)` = ⛔ **`8000`** ⇒ ⛔ **his number is exactly what ships.**
- ⛔⛔ **AND THAT IS ⛔ PRECISELY WHY THE DERIVATION MUST STAY: ⛔ a bare constant re-opens the ⛔ ORDERING INVERSION the moment ⛔ ANY card sets a `NoticeRange` above `5333`.** ⛔ A future `NoticeRange 6000` yields `max(8000, 9000)` = ⛔ `9000` — ⛔ the ordering ⛔ HOLDS ⛔ automatically. ⛔ With a hard-coded `8000` it ⛔ inverts ⛔ silently, and ⛔ `FOG-§9.8b`'s drop-at-`:1713` / re-acquire-at-`:1721` thrash ⛔ returns ⛔ with no diff to point at.
- ⚠️⛔ **THE MULTIPLIER'S ⛔ ORIGINAL JUSTIFICATION IS ⛔ NOW DEAD AND ⛔ MUST BE RE-ARGUED, ⛔ NOT SILENTLY KEPT.** ⛔ `1.5` was **MEASURED**, ⛔ not chosen: at `900/600` it reproduced the shipped ordering ⛔ bit-identically (`max(900, 900) = 900`). ⛔ **At `8000/5000` the ⛔ FLOOR WINS and the multiplier is ⛔ INERT for every shipped card** ⇒ ⛔ its ⛔ only remaining job is the ⛔ FUTURE-CARD case above. ⛔ **Say that in the comment, or the next reader deletes a term that looks like dead code.**
- ✅⛔⛔⭐⭐ **DISCHARGED 2026-09-04 BY ⭐ `TASK-1003` — ⛔ AND IT WENT ⛔ FURTHER THAN THE DEMAND, IN THE ⛔ ONE WAY THAT MATTERS: ⛔ THE JUSTIFICATION IS NOW ⛔ EXECUTED RATHER THAN ⛔ ARGUED.** ⛔ **THIS IS ⛔ SETTLED; ⛔ DO ⛔ NOT RE-OPEN IT.**
  - ⛔ **THE PROBLEM WITH A ⛔ PROSE re-argument: ⛔ deleting the product term would have left ⛔ EVERY TEST GREEN** — ⛔ `max(8000, 7500) == 8000 == 8000`. ⇒ ⛔ **a comment is the ⛔ only thing standing between the term and a ⛔ future tidy-up, and ⛔ comments do not fail builds.**
  - ✅ **THE CROSSOVER IS ⛔ PINNED AT `8000 / 1.5 = 5333.33`** — ⛔ the ⛔ exact notice radius above which the ⛔ PRODUCT overtakes the ⛔ FLOOR ⇒ ⛔ **the term's ⛔ only job is now a ⛔ TESTED job, ⛔ not a described one.**
  - ✅ **AND test 3(f) goes ⛔ RED against a collapse to `return 8000.f;`** ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ exact defect `FOG-§9.11` warned about is ⛔ now caught by a machine ⛔ instead of by a reviewer remembering to look.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ WHEN A TERM BECOMES ⛔ INERT AGAINST TODAY'S DATA, A ⛔ COMMENT EXPLAINING IT IS ⛔ NOT PROTECTION — ⛔ PIN THE ⛔ CROSSOVER WHERE IT ⛔ STOPS BEING INERT. ⛔ THAT CONVERTS *"do not delete this"* FROM A ⛔ REQUEST INTO A ⛔ RED TEST.***

#### ⭐⭐⭐ **THE ORDERING ITSELF — ⛔ THIS IS THE SAFETY PROPERTY, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ THE WHOLE SECTION**

> ⛔⛔⛔ **`LEASH > NOTICE > LONGEST FIRING RANGE`**
> ⛔ **Today: `8000` > `5000` > `3600` (⛔ Longbowman).**

- ⛔ **ANY future card that breaks this ordering ⛔ RE-OPENS THE DROP-THEN-RE-ACQUIRE THRASH.** ⛔ It is ⛔ not a style preference; ⛔ it is the ⛔ reason units disengage at all.
- ⛔⛔ **THE PIN MUST BE ⛔ DERIVED FROM THE DATA, ⛔ NEVER TYPED.** ⛔ A test asserting `8000 > 5000 > 3600` as ⛔ three literals is ⛔ green against a roster it has ⛔ never read. ⛔ **Derive `LONGEST FIRING RANGE` by scanning ⛔ every `bRanged` row of `Docs/Data/cards.csv`, and ⛔ NOTICE by resolving ⛔ every row through `ResolveNoticeRadiusUU`** (⛔ so a populated `NoticeRange` cell is ⛔ caught, ⛔ not assumed absent).
- ⛔ **`SC-§45` applies with full force: ⛔ machine-read the column, ⛔ never the `Notes` prose.**

#### ⛔⛔ **THE RETENTION CLAMP — ⛔ RULED. ⛔ FOG BINDS ⛔ RETENTION, ⛔ NOT ONLY ACQUISITION.**

> 📌 **HIS WORDS: *"yes ⛔ clamp retention under fog."*** ⇒ ⛔ **`J-F23` is ⛔ fully discharged and `TASK-980`'s third blocker is ⛔ CLEARED.**

- ⛔ **An ⛔ UNCOMMANDED unit ⛔ LOSES a target that leaves the ⛔ fog-effective radius.** ⛔ The bound is `ApplyFogVisionCeilingUU(GetEffectiveLeashRangeUU())` — ⛔ **bit-identical in clear weather, `609.6` under fog** — ⛔ **NOT** `min(LeashRange, effective notice)`, which `handoffs/TASK-980-programmer.md` §4(c) ⛔ MEASURED as a clear-weather leash cut ⛔ AND a re-created boundary thrash.
- ✅⛔ **AMBUSH'S UNBOUNDED CHASE IS THE ⛔ DELIBERATE EXCEPTION AND IT ⛔ STAYS.**
- ✅⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CONFIRMED 2026-09-04 BY ⭐ `TASK-1006` item (5) — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ METHOD IS WORTH MORE THAN THE VERDICT.** ⛔ **QA did ⛔ NOT settle it with a ⛔ NAME CENSUS. ⛔ It settled it in the ⛔ CALL GRAPH: the dispatches to `UpdateStateFollow` (`:1619`) and `UpdateStateGrouped` (`:1680`) ⛔ BOTH `return` ⛔ UNCONDITIONALLY ⛔ BEFORE leash site 1** ⇒ ⛔ **HOLD / AMBUSH / FOLLOW ⛔ CANNOT EXECUTE A LEASH READ ON ⛔ ANY PATH.** ⇒ ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1007`/`1008` proceed on the design ⛔ as boarded.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A NAME CENSUS WOULD HAVE RETURNED *"TWO SITES"* AND SAID ⛔ NOTHING ABOUT ⛔ REACHABILITY. ⛔ THE QUESTION *"DOES THIS LANE READ THE LEASH?"* IS A ⛔ CALL-GRAPH QUESTION, AND ⛔ COUNTING SYMBOLS ⛔ ANSWERS A ⛔ DIFFERENT ONE.*** ⭐ **This project's ⛔ SECOND recorded instance of the decisive fact living in the ⛔ CALL GRAPH rather than in any ⛔ diff or ⛔ name list** (⛔ the contact-climb batch was the first, where ⛔ both QA blockers lived there).
- ⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ ORIGINAL WARNING ⛔ STANDS AS WRITTEN — ⛔ RETAINED BECAUSE THE ⛔ INSTRUCTION TO VERIFY IS ⛔ WHAT PRODUCED THE VERDICT:**
- ⭐⭐ **AND THE COLLISION THE HANDOFF FEARED IS ⛔ FAR NARROWER THAN IT ASSUMED — ⛔ MEASURED AT SOURCE, ⛔ AND IT MUST BE ⛔ RE-CONFIRMED BY A GATE BEFORE ANY ROW LEANS ON IT:** ⛔ `UpdateStateGrouped` reads ⛔ **NO leash at all** (`SummonedUnit.cpp:2024` — *"the zones ARE the leash for HOLD, and AMBUSH's whole point is the unbounded chase"*) ⇒ ⛔ **HOLD / AMBUSH / FOLLOW are ⛔ UNTOUCHED by ⛔ any leash change, in ⛔ either direction.** ⛔ **The ⛔ ONLY commanded lane that reads the leash is `UpdateStateStandardCommanded`'s ⛔ ATTACK path (`:1979`, ⛔ site 2 of 2).**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN OBJECTION RAISED AGAINST A ⛔ WHOLE CATEGORY ("commanded units") ⛔ COLLAPSED TO ⛔ ONE CODE PATH THE MOMENT SOMEBODY ⛔ READ THE CATEGORY'S OTHER MEMBERS.*** ⛔ **The handoff's refusal was ⛔ still correct** — ⛔ it refused on a ⛔ fence, ⛔ not on this claim.

#### ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **WHAT THIS ⛔ RETIRES — ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT A DETAIL**

- ⛔⛔ **`FOG-§9.9` / `FOG-§9.10` / `FOG-§9.10a` record *"⛔ notice ⛔ == firing ⛔ for ranged units"* as ⛔ LAW. ⛔ HIS `5000` ⛔ RETIRES IT.** ⛔ Every ranged unit now notices ⛔ well beyond its firing range — ⛔ `Longbowman 5000 / 3600` · ⛔ `Archer 5000 / 2100` · ⛔ `Wizard 5000 / 2100`.
- ✅ **THAT IS ⛔ CORRECT AND ⛔ NORMAL — ⛔ it makes ranged units behave like ⛔ melee ones already do (⛔ notice far, ⛔ close to fire).** ⛔ **But the law must be ⛔ REWRITTEN, ⛔ not left standing ⛔ FALSE.**
- ⭐⭐ **`FOG-§9.10a`'s ⛔ PROHIBITION ⛔ SURVIVES ⛔ INTACT AND IS ⛔ NOW ⛔ STRONGER, ⛔ NOT WEAKER:** ⛔ the identity rule is ⛔ gone, so there is ⛔ no longer ⛔ any unit class for which collapsing notice into firing is ⛔ even approximately right. ⛔ **`609.6` stays a ⛔ SHARED CEILING applied as a ⛔ `min` at ⛔ EVERY site. ⛔ `min(120, 609.6) = 120`. ⛔ Not an assignment. ⛔ Not a clamp-to-fog. ⛔ A `min`.**

#### FOG-§10.3 ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ THREE-STATE MACHINE, ⛔ WRITTEN OUT, ⛔ SO NOBODY IMPLEMENTS A ⛔ FOURTH**

```
        ┌──────────────┐   Fog (50g)      ┌──────────────┐
        │  CLEAR       │ ───────────────► │  FOGGED      │   FogDurationSeconds = 300
        │  (no fog,    │                  │  (fog up,    │   ⇒ expires ⇒ CLEAR
        │   no shield) │ ◄─────────────── │   T counts)  │
        └──────┬───────┘   Fog expires    └──────┬───────┘
               │           OR BrightSun          │ BrightSun (60g)
               │                                 │  ⇒ clears fog AND shields
   BrightSun   │                                 ▼
    (60g)      └──────────────────────────► ┌──────────────┐
                                            │  SHIELDED    │  120 + 60 × floor(H / step)
                                            │  (no fog,    │  ⇒ Fog is REFUSED, no gold
                                            │   shield up) │  ⇒ expires ⇒ CLEAR
                                            └──────────────┘
```
- ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ ONE-WAY DOOR HIS SENTENCE ⛔ EXPLICITLY RULES, AND IT IS THE ⛔ EASIEST THING TO GET WRONG: *"Even when the 'bright sun' fog prevention timer ends, ⛔ the fog that was cleared ⛔ STILL REMAINS CLEAR."*** ⇒ ⛔ **`SHIELDED` ⛔ expires to ⛔ `CLEAR`, ⛔ NEVER back to `FOGGED`.** ⛔ **There is ⛔ NO suspended fog, ⛔ NO paused fog timer, ⛔ NO remembered remainder. ⛔ A "resume the fog" implementation is a ⛔ FAIL against his own words.**
- ⛔ **`Play Again` / match reset ⇒ ⛔ `CLEAR`, ⛔ both timers zeroed** (⛔ the shipped reset law, which already covers freezes and buffs).

#### ⚖️⛔⛔⭐⭐ **FOG-§10.6 — HIS ⛔ FOG-DURING-PREVENTION RULING (2026-09-04). ⛔ `J-F19` IS ⛔ CLOSED. ⛔ THREE PROPERTIES, AND ⛔ COLLAPSING THEM IS THE DEFECT.**

> 📌 **HIS WORDS: *"the player should ⛔ not lose gold, ⛔ not have the card get casted, and instead get a ⛔ message telling them bright sun is still up for '⛔ x' amount of seconds, where the 'x' is the ⛔ ACTUAL amount of time left for the fog prevention."***

##### ⛔⛔⛔ **THE THREE PROPERTIES ARE ⛔ SEPARABLE — ⛔ AND THAT IS ⛔ WHY THEY ARE ⛔ THREE ASSERTIONS, ⛔ NEVER ONE**

| # | property | ⛔ how it fails ⛔ ALONE |
|---|---|---|
| **1** | ⛔ **NO GOLD SPENT** | ⛔ a refusal that ⛔ bills the player ⇒ ⛔ fog becomes the ⛔ ONLY card in the game charging for a refusal |
| **2** | ⛔ **THE CARD IS ⛔ NOT CONSUMED — ⛔ it stays in hand** | ⛔⛔ **a build that ⛔ refunds the gold but ⛔ EATS THE CARD ⛔ satisfies half the ruling** ⇒ ⛔ **a test written against ⛔ either half alone ⛔ PASSES the ⛔ broken build** |
| **3** | ⛔ **A MESSAGE CARRYING THE ⛔ ACTUAL SECONDS REMAINING** | ⛔ a ⛔ static *"Bright Sun is still active"* ⛔ satisfies his ⛔ sentence and ⛔ FAILS his ⛔ ruling |

- ⭐ **PROPERTIES 1 + 2 ARE THE ⛔ SHIPPED `RefuseCardPlay` NET-ZERO DOCTRINE, ⛔ VERBATIM** (⛔ `TASK-819`'s discard-all precedent: ⛔ unaffordable ⇒ ⛔ net-zero, ⛔ reusing the shipped string). ⇒ ⛔ **REUSE THE SHAPE; ⛔ do ⛔ not invent a refusal path.**

##### ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THE *"NOVEL MECHANISM"* CLAIM IS ⛔ REFUTED AT SOURCE — ⛔ MEASURED 2026-09-04, ⛔ AND THE CORRECTION ⛔ SHRINKS THE WORK**

- ⛔ **The circulated claim was *"⛔ every shipped refusal is ⛔ STATIC TEXT ⇒ this is the ⛔ FIRST refusal that must ⛔ format a runtime value."*** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **FALSE, ⛔ by census of `SiegePlayerController.cpp`.**
- ✅⭐ **A ⛔ FORMATTED REFUSAL ⛔ ALREADY SHIPS** — ⛔ `SiegePlayerController.cpp` ~4739: `RefuseCardPlay(CardID, FText::Format(NSLOCTEXT("Siegebound", "CardRefused_MaxStacks", "{0} at max stacks"), …))`. ⛔ **Four more refusals come from ⛔ helper functions (`UnitFootprintRefusalText`, `StackNotStackableRefusalText`, `StackTargetGoneRefusalText`, `StackUpgradeFailedRefusalText`), ⛔ not literals.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **`RefuseCardPlay` ⛔ ALREADY TAKES AN `FText` AND `FText::Format` AT THE CALL SITE IS ⛔ ALREADY THE SHIPPED IDIOM.** ⛔ **There is ⛔ NO new plumbing, ⛔ NO new broadcast, ⛔ NO new widget path to build.** ⭐ **The row is ⛔ one more `FText::Format` call site ⛔ plus an ⛔ accessor — ⛔ that is ⛔ all.**
- ⭐⭐ **BUT THE ⛔ SHARPENED NOVELTY IS ⛔ REAL AND IS THE ⛔ THING TO GUARD: ⛔ `CardRefused_MaxStacks` FORMATS A VALUE THAT IS ⛔ CONSTANT FOR THE CARD (⛔ its `DisplayName`). ⛔ THE COUNTDOWN FORMATS A VALUE THAT ⛔ CHANGES BETWEEN TWO CLICKS ONE SECOND APART.** ⇒ ⛔ **THAT is what has ⛔ no precedent, ⛔ not the formatting.**
- ⛔⛔⛔ **THEREFORE: ⛔ THE VALUE IS ⛔ READ AT ⛔ CLICK TIME, ⛔ NEVER CACHED AT ⛔ CAST TIME.** ⛔ **A remaining-time captured when `BrightSun` was ⛔ played would be ⛔ stale by ⛔ exactly the elapsed duration ⇒ ⛔ the message would ⛔ count down from the ⛔ wrong number, ⛔ or ⛔ not count down at all.** ⭐ **`SC-§37` again: ⛔ a ⛔ single-click test ⛔ cannot tell a ⛔ live read from a ⛔ cached one ⇒ ⛔ the gate needs ⛔ TWO refusals ⛔ separated in time.**

##### ⚠️⛔⛔ **THE SIBLING QUESTION IS ⛔ STILL OPEN — ⛔ THIS RULING MUST ⛔ NOT BE READ AS ANSWERING IT**

- ~~⛔ **`J-F18` — `BrightSun` played ⛔ DURING an active prevention window (⛔ refresh · ⛔ extend · ⛔ refuse) — ⛔ REMAINS OPEN.**~~ ⇒ ✅⚖️ **CLOSED 2026-09-04. ⛔ SEE `FOG-§10.7`.** ⛔ **Struck, ⛔ not deleted (`TL-§5c` cl. 4).**
- ⭐⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ BUILD NOTE THAT MATTERS ⛔ NOW RATHER THAN LATER: ⛔ IF `J-F18` COMES BACK ⛔ "REFUSE", it ⛔ almost certainly wants the ⛔ SAME net-zero, ⛔ non-consuming, ⛔ live-countdown treatment.** ⇒ ⛔ **BUILD THE COUNTDOWN REFUSAL ⛔ ONCE AND ⛔ SHARE IT — ⛔ never twice.** ⛔ **The accessor is the shared part; ⛔ the call site is the cheap part.**
  ⇒ ✅⭐⭐ **THIS PREDICTION ⛔ LANDED, ⛔ WITHIN ONE DAY, AND IT IS ⛔ WHY THE FENCE WAS WRITTEN AS *"build it once"* RATHER THAN *"don't build it"*.** ⛔ **`J-F18` came back ⛔ REFUSE-IN-ONE-DIRECTION (`FOG-§10.7`) and the ⛔ shared helper `TASK-989` was told to write ⛔ now has its ⛔ second caller (⭐ `TASK-991`).** ⚖️ ***A fence that says "make the second caller cheap" costs ⛔ nothing when the second caller never arrives, and ⛔ saves a rewrite when it does.*** ⛔ **`SC-§40` cl. 2's no-dead-surface ban was ⛔ never in tension with it: ⛔ ONE helper with ⛔ ONE caller was always legal.**

#### ⚖️⛔⛔⭐⭐ **FOG-§10.7 — THE 2026-09-04 `BrightSun` RULINGS: ⛔ THE DATUM · ⛔ THE SAMPLE · ⛔ FOG-ON-FOG · ⛔ SUN-ON-SUN. ⛔ FOUR CLOSED IN ⛔ ONE MESSAGE.**

> 📌 **HIS WORDS, VERBATIM:** *"that message I just gave about bright sun still being up is ⛔ only for when the 'fog' card gets played. If bright sun is played during the bright sun window, then the timer gets ⛔ reset to whatever the new time would be under the new cast, ⛔ unless that new time would be ⛔ less than the current time, then the player will just get a message that says '⛔ using bright sun right now would reduce fog prevention time from "x" time to "y" time' where 'x' is the ⛔ current time left and 'y' is the ⛔ new fog prevention time under the current height calculation, and the player is ⛔ basically prevented from playing the card. If ⛔ fog is played during fog then the timer is ⛔ reset to 5 minutes. For calculating 'bright sun' fog prevention time, the height is ⛔ sampled at the time that the card is cast. The ground is simply the elevation of the ⛔ flat grass terrain, ⛔ not including the hills, therefore that ground height number should be the ⛔ same anywhere on the map, and it should be the ⛔ same height in which ranged units' damage is at ⛔ 1 times their damage and increases by 10% every ⛔ 5 feet above that."*

##### ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(A) `J-F18` — SUN-ON-SUN IS ⛔ CONDITIONAL. ⛔ NOT `max`, ⛔ NOT refresh, ⛔ NOT refuse — ⛔ A ⛔ BRANCH.**

| the new window vs the remaining one | ⛔ what happens |
|---|---|
| ⛔ **LONGER** | ⛔ **RESET to the new duration.** ⛔ Gold spent, ⛔ card consumed, ⛔ normal cast |
| ⛔ **SHORTER** (⛔ or equal — ⛔ see the boundary note) | ⛔⛔ **REFUSED. ⛔ NO GOLD SPENT · ⛔ CARD ⛔ NOT CONSUMED · ⛔ message: *"using bright sun right now would reduce fog prevention time from X to Y"*** |

- ⭐⭐ **THE OLD DEFAULT WAS ⛔ RIGHT ABOUT THE ⛔ DURATION AND ⛔ WRONG ABOUT THE ⛔ COST, AND THAT IS THE ⛔ WHOLE DELTA.** ⛔ `max(remaining, new)` and *"reset if longer"* produce the ⛔ IDENTICAL remaining time in the ⛔ longer case. ⇒ ⛔ **they diverge ⛔ ONLY in the ⛔ shorter case, where `max` would have ⛔ silently kept the timer while ⛔ BILLING the player 60 gold and ⛔ EATING the card.** ⚖️ ***The default's ⛔ arithmetic was correct and its ⛔ economics were not — and ⛔ no test of the resulting duration could ever have caught that.***
- ⛔⛔ **THIS IS THE ⛔ SAME THREE-PROPERTY SHAPE AS `J-F19` (`FOG-§10.6`): ⛔ no gold · ⛔ card retained · ⛔ a message carrying ⛔ LIVE values.** ⇒ ⛔ **the two refusals are ⛔ SIBLINGS and ⛔ share their machinery.**
- ⛔ **BUT THEY ⛔ DIFFER IN ⛔ ARITY, AND THAT DECIDES ⛔ WHAT IS ACTUALLY SHARED:** `J-F19`'s message carries ⛔ **ONE** value; `J-F18`'s carries ⛔ **TWO**. ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE SHARED THING IS THE ⛔ SECONDS→`FText` FORMATTER (⛔ one scalar in, ⛔ one `FText` out), ⛔ CALLED ⛔ ONCE BY ONE MESSAGE AND ⛔ TWICE BY THE OTHER. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOT A SHARED MESSAGE BUILDER — ⛔ a builder cannot span two arities and an attempt to make it will produce an optional-second-value parameter that is ⛔ dead half the time.**
- ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THE MECHANISM CONSEQUENCE THAT IS ⛔ EASY TO MISS AND IS A ⛔ SEAM REQUIREMENT, ⛔ NOT AN IMPLEMENTATION DETAIL: ⛔ `Y` IS *"the new fog prevention time ⛔ UNDER THE CURRENT HEIGHT CALCULATION"* ⇒ ⛔ THE REFUSAL MUST ⛔ COMPUTE A ⛔ FULL `BrightSun` DURATION ⛔ JUST TO EXPLAIN WHY IT REFUSES.** ⇒ ⛔ **THE DURATION CALCULATION MUST BE ⛔ CALLABLE ⛔ OUTSIDE THE CAST PATH.** ⛔ **A duration computed ⛔ inside `ApplyBrightSun` (or ⛔ inline in the cast branch) ⛔ cannot be reached by the refusal ⛔ without either ⛔ duplicating the formula or ⛔ casting the card to find out whether to cast it.** ⭐ **Both of those are ⛔ defects; ⛔ the seam is the ⛔ fix, and it must be ⛔ boarded as a ⛔ REQUIREMENT.**
- ⛔ **AND `Y` DEPENDS ON HERO POSITION ⛔ AT THAT INSTANT** ⇒ ⛔ **BOTH values are ⛔ LIVE. ⛔ `X` is the live remainder (⛔ `FOG-§10.6`'s law, ⛔ unchanged); ⛔ `Y` is a live height sample.** ⛔ **Neither may be cached at cast time.**
- ⚠️ **THE ⛔ EQUAL BOUNDARY IS A ⛔ DECLARED DEFAULT, ⛔ NOT HIS WORD: he wrote *"⛔ unless that new time would be ⛔ LESS than the current time"*.** ⇒ ⛔ **EQUAL is ⛔ not "less" ⇒ ⛔ EQUAL ⛔ RESETS (i.e. it is a ⛔ legal, ⛔ if pointless, cast).** ⛔ **Ship the ⛔ strict `new < remaining ⇒ refuse` reading — ⛔ it is the ⛔ literal one — and ⛔ note that a float-equal window is ⛔ unreachable in practice.**

##### ✅ **(B) `J-F15` — SAMPLED ⛔ AT CAST TIME. ⛔ THE DEFAULT WAS RIGHT; ⛔ NOTHING UNWINDS.**
##### ✅ **(C) `J-F16` — FOG ON FOG ⇒ ⛔ RESET TO `300` s. ⛔ THE DEFAULT WAS RIGHT; ⛔ NOTHING UNWINDS.** ⭐ **His sentence ⛔ independently re-derives `FogDurationSeconds == 300.f` — ⛔ the same number from ⛔ two different sentences of his, ⛔ which is the ⛔ strongest confirmation a tunable ever gets here.**

##### ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(D) `J-F13` — THE DATUM. ⛔ ONE CONSTANT, ⛔ TWO CONSUMERS — AND THE ⛔ MEASUREMENT SAYS THE SECOND CONSUMER ⛔ DOES NOT EXIST YET.**

> ⚖️ **HIS RULING JOINS TWO NUMBERS THAT WERE NEVER JOINED:** *"that ground height number should be the ⛔ same anywhere on the map, and it should be the ⛔ SAME HEIGHT IN WHICH RANGED UNITS' DAMAGE IS AT ⛔ 1 TIMES THEIR DAMAGE."*

- ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **MEASURED AT SOURCE 2026-09-04, AND IT ⛔ REFUTES THE PREMISE OF THE REUSE: ⛔ THERE IS ⛔ NO ELEVATION-DAMAGE ZERO CONSTANT. ⛔ THERE IS ⛔ NO NUMBER TO REUSE.**
  `ASummonedUnit::ComputeOutputDamage` (`SummonedUnit.cpp:4418-4425`) calls `HeightAdvantageMultiplier(GetActorLocation().Z, ⛔ Target->GetActorLocation().Z, …)`. ⇒ ⛔ **the damage zero is the ⛔ TARGET'S OWN Z, ⛔ read from the actor, ⛔ recomputed per attack, ⛔ per pair.** ⛔ **`HIGH-§2` row `R-1` ⛔ rules this ⛔ deliberately: *"above the target, ⛔ NOT absolute world Z"*, and `SummonedUnit.cpp:4331` says so in the code.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE DAMAGE LANE'S ×1.0 IS A ⛔ RELATIVE CONDITION (⛔ attacker Z ≤ target Z), ⛔ TRUE AT ⛔ ANY ABSOLUTE ELEVATION.** ⛔ **A Longbowman on a 2,400 uu tower shooting a target ⛔ on that same tower deals ⛔ ×1.0.** ⇒ ⛔ **his sentence describes a datum the damage lane ⛔ does not possess.**
- ✅⭐ **BUT THE NUMBER HE MEANS ⛔ DOES EXIST AND IS ⛔ ALREADY PINNED: ⛔ `Z = 0`.** `CONVENTIONS:131` pins `SM_ArenaTerrain` as *"placed at (0,0,0) it reproduces the old ArenaGround slab's ⛔ walk surface at Z=0"*, and ⛔ **`FSiegeAssistantSnapshot::MarkPlaceGroundZ = 0.f` (`SiegeAssistantSnapshot.h:831`) ⛔ ALREADY CARRIES THAT EXACT FACT**, with the derivation written above it.
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THE DRIFT SURFACE HE IS WORRIED ABOUT ⛔ ALREADY EXISTS: the arena-floor datum is ⛔ hand-typed as `0.f` in ⛔ at least one shipped header ⛔ today, and `BrightSun` would make it ⛔ two.**

###### ⛔ **THE LAW — HOW IT IS BUILT**

| # | ⛔ rule |
|---|---|
| **1** | ⛔ **ONE named constant: ⭐ `ArenaGroundReferenceZUU = 0.f`**, `EditDefaultsOnly`, with its `HIGH-§1` consequence written beside it. ⛔ **It is the elevation of the ⛔ FLAT GRASS TERRAIN. ⛔ It is ⛔ NOT traced, ⛔ NOT sampled, ⛔ NOT read from any actor** |
| **2** | ⛔ **CONSUMER 1 (⛔ runtime): `BrightSun`'s height zero** ⇒ `Height = HeroZ − ArenaGroundReferenceZUU`, ⛔ clamped to ≥ 0 steps |
| **3** | ⛔⛔ **CONSUMER 2 (⛔ ASSERTION, ⛔ NOT a runtime read): a ⛔ PINNED TEST tying the damage lane to the ⛔ SAME symbol** — ⛔ `HeightAdvantageMultiplier(ArenaGroundReferenceZUU, ArenaGroundReferenceZUU, Step, Bonus) == ⛔ 1.0` ⛔ exactly, and ⛔ `(ArenaGroundReferenceZUU + HeightBonusStepUU, ArenaGroundReferenceZUU, …) == ⛔ 1.10`. ⭐ **That is ⛔ HIS SENTENCE MADE EXECUTABLE, and it goes ⛔ RED if ⛔ either lane's datum moves** |
| **4** | ⛔⛔⛔ **THE DAMAGE FORMULA IS ⛔ NOT CHANGED. ⛔ NOT ONE LINE.** ⛔ **Converting it to read `ArenaGroundReferenceZUU` at runtime would be a ⛔ GAME-WIDE REBALANCE (⛔ every attacker on a hill would gain a bonus against a target ⛔ on that same hill) and would ⛔ contradict `HIGH-§2` `R-1`, ⛔ his own earlier ruling.** ⇒ ⛔ **the tie is an ⛔ ASSERTION, ⛔ never a ⛔ call** |
| **5** | ⛔ **DO ⛔ NOT reuse `FSiegeAssistantSnapshot::MarkPlaceGroundZ`.** ⛔ Its own comment licenses it to be ⛔ retuned for hill-accurate marks — ⛔ the ⛔ exact `FOG-§9.5` trap (⛔ never couple a card to a constant somebody else may retune for an unrelated reason). ⛔ **Its `0.f` is ⛔ REPORTED as a third site, ⛔ NOT swept** |

- ⭐⭐ **WHY THE OLD DEFAULT WAS REFUTED, AND IT IS THE ⛔ MOST INSTRUCTIVE FAILURE IN THIS LANE: ⛔ `J-F13`'s argument ⛔ EXPLICITLY CONSIDERED AND ⛔ REJECTED *"a pinned ground-plane Z"* — on the grounds that it *"⛔ breaks on the M6.5 hills"* — and ⛔ HE THEN RULED ⛔ EXACTLY THAT OPTION, ⛔ for exactly the behaviour the argument called breakage.** ⚖️ ***The default reasoned correctly from an assumption about his intent that it had ⛔ no way to check, and ⛔ stated the assumption plainly enough that his one sentence could ⛔ overturn it. ⛔ That is the fence working, ⛔ not failing.***
- ⛔ **A hero on a hill ⛔ EARNS the height. ⛔ He earns the damage bonus there too. ⛔ The two are now consistent ⛔ by his ruling, and ⛔ rule 3 is what ⛔ keeps them consistent.**

##### ✅⛔⛔ **(E) `HeightBonusStepUU` — ⛔ NO CHANGE. ⛔ THE `10 ft` IN HIS MESSAGE WAS A ⛔ MISSPEAK, ⛔ CONFIRMED BY HIM.**

- ⛔ **His sentence read *"increases by 10% every ⛔ 10 feet above that"*. ⛔ Shipped source is `SummonedUnit.h:1260` — `float HeightBonusStepUU = 152.4f; // HIGH-§1: ⛔ 5 ft × 30.48 cm/ft`.**
- ⛔ **The conflict was ⛔ MEASURED and ⛔ PUT TO HIM before a line moved. ⛔ He answered: *"keep it as a 10% increase in damage every ⛔ 5 feet above the ground, I ⛔ mispoke when I said 10 feet."*** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE SHIPPED VALUE IS ⛔ CORRECT. ⛔ DO ⛔ NOT TOUCH IT. ⛔ `152.4f` STANDS.**
- ⭐⭐⭐ **RECORD ⛔ WHY THIS ROW IS HERE AT ALL, BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ CHEAPEST WIN OF THE DAY: ⛔ TAKING THE SENTENCE AT FACE VALUE WOULD HAVE ⛔ DOUBLED THE STEP AND ⛔ HALVED EVERY ELEVATION DAMAGE BONUS IN THE GAME — ⛔ a ⛔ silent, ⛔ game-wide rebalance, ⛔ landing green, ⛔ with a ⛔ test suite that would have been ⛔ dutifully updated to match.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE MEASUREMENT TURNED A GAME-WIDE REBALANCE INTO A ⛔ TWO-LINE EXCHANGE.*** ⛔ **Generalise it: ⛔ when a design sentence ⛔ contradicts a ⛔ shipped, ⛔ playtested, ⛔ commented constant, the ⛔ contradiction itself is the deliverable — ⛔ quote both numbers to him and ⛔ change nothing** (`SC-§45`'s family; ⛔ the `13`-vs-`16` and `Lightning` `400`-vs-`700` precedents).

- ⛔⛔ **WHAT PROPAGATING THEM WOULD HAVE COST, ⛔ ROW BY ROW — ⛔ and it is ⛔ worse than a typo, because ⛔ each error does ⛔ TWO kinds of damage at once:**

|---|---|---|

- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A RULING IS ⛔ IDENTIFIED BY ITS ⛔ QUESTION, ⛔ NEVER BY ITS ⛔ NUMBER. ⛔ MATCH HIS WORDS TO THE ⛔ QUESTION TEXT IN THE TABLE BEFORE FLIPPING A ⛔ STATUS — ⛔ the ID is a ⛔ label, and ⛔ labels are exactly what drifts.*** ⭐ **This is the ⛔ `SC-§56`/nine-site-table family arriving in the ⛔ RULING LEDGER rather than in code, and the ledger is ⛔ where it is ⛔ hardest to detect: ⛔ a wrongly-closed ruling ⛔ looks identical to a rightly-closed one.**
